Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Planning for real-world problems by language models often involves both world and user constraints, which may not be fully specified upfront and are progressively disclosed through interaction. However, existing benchmarks still underexplore adaptive planning under such progressively revealed dual constraints. To address this gap, we introduce AdaPlanBench, a dynamic interactive benchmark for evaluating whether Large Language Model (LLM) agents can adaptively plan and re-plan under progressively revealed world and user constraints. AdaPlanBench is built on 307 household tasks, with a scalable constraint construction pipeline that augments each task with dual constraints. At runtime, agents interact with the environment in a multi-turn protocol where hidden constraints are revealed only when the agent proposes a plan that violates them, requiring iterative plan revision under accumulating feedback. This makes planning challenging, as agents must infer and track constraints from feedback while re-planning effectively. Experiments on ten leading LLMs show that adaptive planning under dual constraints remains challenging, with the best model reaching only 67.75% accuracy. We further observe that performance degrades as more constraints accumulate, with user constraints posing a particularly large challenge and failures often stemming from weaker physical grounding and reduced effectiveness. These results establish AdaPlanBench as a testbed for dual-constrained interactive planning and highlight the challenge of reliable adaptation to dynamically revealed constraints in LLM agents.

</details>


### [30] [Bootstrapping Semantic Layer from Execution for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05634)
*Youngwon Lee,Jaejin Kim,Seung-won Hwang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GATE提出了一种基于执行反馈的文本到SQL系统，通过在执行过程中保持假设开放并利用观察结果来引导 grounding，从而构建可重用的执行 grounded 记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的文本到SQL任务往往在用户短语与数据库存储值建立grounding之前是未充分指定的。先前工作要求预先定义语义层来指定grounding，但这些规范通常是不完整的，特别是在领域特定约定缺乏文档记录的专业领域。这导致对于同一个SQL部分存在多个grounding假设。

Method: GATE（Grounding After Test from Execution）从执行反馈中引导缺失的grounding。它保持grounding假设开放，同时执行已经grounded的部分以获取观察结果。只有被该观察结果支持的假设才会被grounding并存储为记忆条目，记录测试内容以及开放部分在SQL中应如何编写。这些条目累积成执行grounded记忆，允许后续步骤重用支持的grounding。

Result: 在真实世界和受控基准测试上，GATE持续强于强大的基线。实验证明执行可以不仅作为验证手段，还可以作为可重用记忆的引导机制。

Conclusion: 执行反馈能够为文本到SQL创建可重用的记忆，grounding after test 是一种有效的方法，证明了执行在系统中的双重作用。

Abstract: Real-world text-to-SQL is often under-specified until user phrases are grounded in how the database stores values. Prior work attempts to address this by requiring a semantic layer to specify groundings in advance, but such specifications are often incomplete, especially in expert domains where domain-specific conventions are under-documented. As this leaves multiple grounding hypotheses open for the same SQL part, we introduce GATE (Grouding After Test from Execution), which bootstraps missing groundings from execution feedback. GATE keeps grounding hypotheses open while executing the already grounded parts to obtain observations. Then, only the hypothesis supported by that observation is grounded and stored as a memory entry, recording what was tested and how the open part should be written in SQL. These entries accumulate into execution-grounded memory, allowing later steps to reuse supported groundings. Across real-world and controlled benchmarks, GATE consistently improves over strong baselines, demonstrating that execution can serve not only as validation but also as a bootstrapping mechanism for reusable memory in text-to-SQL.

</details>


### [31] [Beyond tokens: a unified framework for latent communication in LLM-based multi-agent systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05711)
*Yingzhuo Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Multi-agent systems built on large language models (LLMs) have become a prevailing paradigm for tackling complex reasoning, planning, and tool-use tasks. The dominant communication protocol in such systems is natural language: agents exchange messages token-by-token, verbalising their internal reasoning so that peers can read, verify, and respond. While convenient and interpretable, this protocol suffers from three structural drawbacks -- high inference cost, irreversible information loss during discretization, and ambiguity/redundancy of natural language. A growing body of work therefore explores an alternative protocol -- latent communication -- in which agents exchange continuous representations (embeddings, hidden states, or KV-caches) directly, bypassing the bottleneck of text generation. This paper presents a unified framework for organising the rapidly expanding literature on latent communication. We analyse existing methods along three orthogonal axes: (1) WHAT information is communicated (Embeddings, Hidden States, KV-Caches, or other continuous state); (2) WHICH sender-receiver alignment is used (latent-space alignment and layer alignment); and (3) HOW the communicated information is fused into the receiver (concatenation, prepending, mathematical operations, cross-attention, or cache restoration). Under this 3-axis framework, we systematically categorise eighteen representative methods proposed between 2024 and 2026, identify five major design patterns, and surface a set of open challenges -- including cross-architecture alignment, security of latent channels, compression for edge deployment, and the relationship between latent communication and latent chain-of-thought. We hope that this framework both lowers the barrier to entry for new researchers and provides a vocabulary for comparing future work.

</details>


### [32] [MARDoc: A Memory-Aware Refinement Agent Framework for Multimodal Long Document QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05749)
*Kaifeng Chen,Hongtao Liu,Qiyao Peng,Jian Yang,Yongqiang Liu,Xiaochen Zhang,Qing Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MARDoc提出一种基于结构化记忆的框架，通过三个专用智能体（探索者、精炼者、反思者）协作处理多模态长文档问答，有效降低上下文噪声，提升多跳推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代检索-推理系统维护单一累积上下文，混合检索轨迹、观察和中间推理，导致关键证据分散稀释，多跳推理噪声增加。

Method: 将长文档QA分解为三个专用智能体：Explorer（多粒度多模态检索）、Refiner（将交互轨迹提炼为结构化证据和推理记忆）、Reflector（检查证据充分性并提供针对性反馈）。各智能体依赖动态更新的结构化记忆而非完整交互历史。

Result: 在MMLongBench-Doc和DocBench上的实验表明，MARDoc在相同骨干模型基线上表现更优，验证了结构化记忆在智能体文档问答中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该设计在保留答案关键事实及其逻辑依赖的同时减少了上下文噪声，证明了结构化记忆对于提升多跳推理质量的重要作用。

Abstract: Iterative retrieval-reasoning agents have recently shown promise for multimodal long-document question answering. However, most existing systems maintain a single growing context that mixes retrieval traces, observations, and intermediate reasoning. As interactions accumulate, key evidence becomes scattered and diluted, making multi-hop reasoning noisy. We propose MARDoc, a Memory-Aware Refinement Agent framework that decouples long-document QA into three specialized agents: an Explorer for multi-granularity multimodal retrieval, a Refiner for distilling interaction traces into structured evidence and reasoning memories, and a Reflector for checking evidence sufficiency and providing targeted feedback. Across iterations, the agents rely on a dynamically updated structured memory rather than a full accumulated interaction history. This design reduces context noise while preserving answer-critical facts and their logical dependencies. Experiments on MMLongBench-Doc and DocBench show that MARDoc achieves strong results, outperforming same-backbone baselines and demonstrating the effectiveness of structured memory for agentic document QA.

</details>


### [33] [CollabBench: Benchmarking and Unleashing Collaborative Ability of LLMs with Diverse Players via Proactive Engagement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05793)
*Hong Qian,Yuanhao Liu,Zihan Zhou,Zongbao Zhang,Hanjie Ge,Haotian Shi,Liang Dou,Xiangfeng Wang,Jingwen Yang,Aimin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CollabBench是一个用于评估和训练协作代理的基准测试，通过多样化玩家画像模拟和协作代理训练范式，在合作游戏中实现了19.5%的效率提升和24.4%的情感表现改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在个人任务上表现出色，但与真实人类伙伴的有效协作仍具挑战性。现有对话级协作研究缺乏基于的交互和行为执行，因此需要协作游戏环境来支持上下文化和沉浸式协作。

Method: 提出CollabBench基准测试，包含：1) Diverse Player Profile Simulation管道用于模拟多样化玩家行为；2) Collaborative Agentic Training范式，统一推理、沟通和行动；3) 使用混合奖励平衡任务效率和情感适应；4) 扩展经典环境为CWAH-MultiPlayer和Cook-MultiPlayer。

Result: 训练后的模型在效率上比基线模型高出19.5%，情感表现提高了24.4%。实验结果验证了CollabBench的有效性。

Conclusion: 分析揭示了现有模型的关键协作局限性，为未来协作训练提供了重要见解，证明了协作游戏环境在提升协作代理能力方面的价值。

Abstract: While LLM-based agents excel at individual tasks, effective collaboration with realistic human partners remains challenging. Most of the existing conversation-level collaborative studies lack grounded interaction and behavioral execution, motivating the need for cooperative game environments that enable contextualized and immersive collaboration. To this end, this paper proposes CollabBench, a benchmark for evaluating and training collaborative agents in cooperative games. CollabBench features a Diverse Player Profile Simulation pipeline to model varied players behaviors, and a Collaborative Agentic Training paradigm that unifies reasoning, communication, and action via agentic rollouts, optimized with a hybrid reward balancing task efficiency and affective adaptation. We further extend classic environments to CWAH-MultiPlayer and Cook-MultiPlayer for systematic evaluation under diverse personalities. Experiments with efficiency and affective metrics show that our trained models outperform base models, achieving 19.5% higher efficiency and 24.4% improved affective performance. Further analysis reveals key collaborative limitations of existing models and offers insights for future collaborative training.

</details>


### [34] [Can LLMs Be Constrained to the Past? Improving Knowledge Cutoff through Recall-Based Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05804)
*Michiro Asai,Ailiang Lin,Yu Kishimoto,Takao Obi,Satoshi Kosugi,Kotaro Funakoshi,Manabu Okumura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于回忆的提示策略（Self-Recall和Question-Recall）来提升大语言模型的知识截止性能，通过要求模型重述截止约束或回忆问题相关信息，在多种基准测试上均优于直接回答和传统逐步推理方法，特别是在反事实问题上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识截止方法主要依赖直接回答生成，当后截止知识仅与问题有因果关联而非明确查询时效果不佳。

Method: 提出两种基于回忆的提示策略：Self-Recall（要求模型重述其截止约束）和Question-Recall（要求模型回忆截止日期内有效的相关问题信息）。

Result: 在三个现有基准测试上，方法优于直接回答提示和传统逐步推理基线，尤其在反事实问题上提升显著。构建了多截止日期历史事件基准（MHEB），发现性能随截止距离变化，且SR与QR组合效果最佳。

Conclusion: 知识截止性能受截止日期距离影响，结合Self-Recall和Question-Recall能持续获得最佳性能，验证了基于回忆提示策略的有效性。

Abstract: Prompted knowledge cutoff instructs a large language model (LLM) to act as if information beyond a specified cutoff date were unavailable. However, prior work mainly relies on direct-answer generation, which struggles when post-cutoff knowledge is not explicitly queried but is only causally related to the question. To address this limitation, we propose two recall-based prompting strategies: Self-Recall (SR), which asks the model to restate its cutoff constraint, and Question-Recall (QR), which requires the model to recall question-relevant information valid under the cutoff. Across three existing benchmarks, our methods outperform both direct-answer prompting and conventional step-by-step reasoning baselines, with particularly strong improvements on counterfactual questions. To investigate robustness across different cutoff settings, we further construct the Multi-cutoff Historical Event Benchmark (MHEB), which evaluates the same question under multiple cutoff years. Results show that knowledge cutoff performance varies with cutoff distance, while combining SR and QR consistently yields the best performance.

</details>


### [35] [ProSPy: A Profiling-Driven SQL-Python Agentic Framework for Enterprise Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05836)
*Zhaorui Yang,Huawei Zheng,Sen Yang,Yuhui Zhang,Haoxuan Li,Zhizhen Yu,Xuan Yi,Chen Hou,Defeng Xie,Chao Hu,Minfeng Zhu,Dazhen Deng,Haozhe Feng,Danqing Huang,Yingcai Wu,Peng Chen,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ProSPy是一个面向企业级Text-to-SQL的框架，通过SQL和Python的协同工作，解决大型异构数据库、元数据不完整和SQL方言差异等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在Text-to-SQL方面取得进展，但在企业级数据库应用中仍面临挑战：大型异构schema、元数据不完整、特定SQL方言语法和复杂分析问题。

Method: ProSPy采用四阶段推理流程：1) 通过自动配置提取细粒度数据证据；2) 逐步将大型schema修剪为任务相关上下文；3) 通过方言无关SQL接口获取中间视图；4) 使用Python执行灵活的下游分析。

Result: 在Spider 2.0-Lite和Spider 2.0-Snow数据集上，ProSPy consistently优于强基线模型，使用Claude-4.5-Opus达到60.15%和60.51%的执行准确率（无需多数投票）。

Conclusion: ProSPy对SQL方言变化具有鲁棒性，在schema召回率和精确度之间实现了良好的权衡，结合了SQL的高效性和Python的灵活性，减少了依赖不可靠元数据，提高了跨SQL方言的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models have substantially advanced Text-to-SQL systems, yet applying them to enterprise-scale databases remains challenging. Real-world databases often contain large and heterogeneous schemas, incomplete metadata, dialect-specific SQL syntax, and complex analytical questions that are difficult to solve with a single SQL query. To address these challenges, we propose ProSPy, a Profiling-driven SQL--Python agentic framework for enterprise-scale Text-to-SQL. ProSPy structures the reasoning process into four stages: it first extracts fine-grained data evidence through automatic profiling, progressively prunes large schemas into task-relevant contexts, fetches intermediate views through a dialect-agnostic SQL interface, and finally performs flexible downstream analysis with Python. This design combines the efficiency of SQL over large databases with the flexibility of Python-based analysis, while reducing reliance on unreliable metadata and improving robustness across SQL dialects. Experiments on Spider 2.0-Lite and Spider 2.0-Snow show that ProSPy consistently outperforms strong baselines with both open-source and proprietary models, achieving execution accuracies of 60.15% and 60.51% with Claude-4.5-Opus, without majority voting. Further analysis shows that ProSPy is robust to SQL dialect variations and achieves a favorable trade-off between schema recall and precision.

</details>


### [36] [Mechanistic Insights into Functional Sparsity in Multimodal LLMs via CoRe Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05843)
*Ruoxi Sun,Quantong Qiu,Juntao Li,Zecheng Tang,Yihang Lou,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过引入检索注意力质量(RAM)指标，在多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)中发现了功能稀疏性这一结构特性，并识别出负责上下文感知检索的专用头，证明了其对于模型性能和推理加速的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MLLMs在复杂视觉-语言任务中表现出色，但它们从复杂噪声上下文中提取查询相关视觉特征的机制仍不透明。研究人员旨在深入探究MLLMs内部的结构特性，揭示跨模态检索的工作原理。

Method: 开发了一种名为检索注意力质量(RAM)的token级指标来分析注意力头。通过该指标识别并表征了"上下文感知检索(CoRe)头"，并在多种视觉领域和模型规模上进行了因果干预和消融实验。

Result: 研究发现CoRe头作为专用信息提取器，与其他广泛分布注意力的头形成功能分工。仅移除前5%的CoRe头就会显著降低多模态推理性能，而移除较低排名的头影响很小。利用这种局部稀疏性可以显著加速推理同时保持鲁棒的任务性能。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了MLLMs中功能稀疏性的结构原理，细化了对机制可解释性的理解，为未来架构设计和模型优化提供了理论基础。该发现有助于解释MLLMs如何从复杂视觉上下文中有效提取相关信息。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) demonstrate remarkable proficiency on complex vision-language tasks, the mechanisms by which they extract query-relevant visual features from complex, noisy contexts remain opaque. In this paper, we present an in-depth interpretability study that uncovers a profound structural property within MLLMs: functional sparsity in cross-modal retrieval. Leveraging a token-level metric termed Retrieval Attention Mass (RAM), we identify and characterize a highly specialized subset of attention heads, referred to as Context-aware Retrieval (CoRe) heads. Across diverse visual domains and model scales, we observe a clear functional division: CoRe heads act as dedicated information extractors, while most other heads distribute attention over broader contextual regions. Causal interventions further demonstrate the necessity of these specialized heads. Ablating only the top 5% of CoRe heads causes significant degradation in multimodal reasoning performance, whereas ablating lower-ranked heads has minimal effect. Moreover, acceleration experiments validate the utility of CoRe heads, showing that leveraging this localized sparsity significantly accelerates inference while maintaining robust task performance. Our findings reveal a structural principle of functional sparsity within MLLMs, refining the current understanding of mechanistic interpretability and laying a theoretical foundation that can inspire future architecture design and model optimization.

</details>


### [37] [ReverseEOL: Improving Training-free Text Embeddings via Text Reversal in Decoder-only LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05858)
*Ailiang Lin,Zhuoyun Li,Yusong Wang,Keyu Mao,Kotaro Funakoshi,Manabu Okumura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ReverseEOL方法，通过结合前向和反向嵌入来增强冻结LLM的表示能力，在STS和MTEB基准测试上显著提升训练-free基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解码器-only大语言模型采用因果注意力机制，导致早期token无法关注未来上下文，从而产生有偏见的上下文表示。

Method: ReverseEOL方法在标准前向嵌入基础上增加一个由反向输入文本派生的反向嵌入。通过反转输入使每个token都能接触到原始顺序中不可访问的上下文，提供互补信息。

Result: 在STS和MTEB基准测试上，ReverseEOL显著提升了多种架构和规模LLM的训练-free基线性能。消融实验和进一步分析证实了反转机制的必要性。

Conclusion: 简单有效的ReverseEOL方法通过结合前向和反向嵌入，有效解决了因果注意力机制导致的表示偏见问题，在多个基准测试中取得了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have opened new avenues for generating training-free text embeddings. However, the causal attention in decoder-only LLMs prevents earlier tokens from attending to future context, leading to biased contextualized representations. In this work, we propose Reverse prompting with Explicit One-word Limitation (ReverseEOL), a simple yet effective method for enhancing the representational capability of frozen LLMs. ReverseEOL augments the standard forward embedding with an additional reversed embedding derived from the reversed input text. Since reversing the input exposes each token to context inaccessible in the original order, the resulting reversed embedding effectively provides complementary information to the original one. As a result, combining the forward and reversed embeddings yields a richer final representation. Comprehensive experiments on STS and MTEB benchmarks demonstrate that ReverseEOL significantly improves the performance of existing training-free baselines across a broad range of LLMs with diverse architectures and scales. Extensive ablations and analyses further confirm the necessity of our reversal mechanism.

</details>


### [38] [TARPO: Token-Wise Latent-Explicit Reasoning via Action-Routing Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05859)
*Liting Zhang,Shiwan Zhao,Xuyang Zhao,Zichen Xu,Jianye Wang,Qicheng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TARPO，一种纯强化学习框架，通过轻量级动作头路由器在每个步骤自适应切换离散token生成与连续潜在推理，以增强LLM的推理能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续潜在推理虽表达能力更强，但具有确定性限制了强化学习中的策略探索；现有方法要么仅使用离散CoT，要么仅使用潜在推理，缺乏灵活性

Method: TARPO采用轻量级动作头路由器，基于当前隐藏状态从二元模式选择空间采样路由决策，使LLM主干和路由器联合端到端优化，共享组相对优势信号，在每步自适应切换离散token生成和连续潜在推理

Result: 在Qwen2.5（1.5B至7B）和Llama-3.1-8B上的广泛实验表明，TARPO在多个基准测试中持续优于现有的显式和潜在推理RL基线，并学习到自适应的token级切换行为同时保持稳定的训练动态

Conclusion: TARPO证明了通过自适应切换离散和潜在推理可以提升LLM推理性能，同时保持训练稳定性，代码已开源

Abstract: Latent reasoning has emerged as a promising alternative to discrete Chain-of-Thought (CoT) in large language models (LLMs), enabling more expressive reasoning by operating over continuous representations. However, the inherently deterministic nature of continuous representations limits policy exploration in reinforcement learning (RL). To address this, we propose TARPO (Token-Wise Latent-Explicit Reasoning via Action-Routing Policy Optimization), a pure RL framework that adaptively switches between discrete token generation and continuous latent reasoning at each step. TARPO introduces a lightweight action head router that observes the current hidden state and samples a routing decision from a binary mode-selection space, preserving the stochasticity of discrete token sampling from the vocabulary. The LLM backbone and router are jointly optimized end-to-end with a shared group-relative advantage signal. Extensive experiments across Qwen2.5 (from 1.5B to 7B) and Llama-3.1-8B backbones demonstrate that TARPO consistently outperforms existing explicit and latent reasoning RL baselines across diverse benchmarks. Further analysis shows that TARPO learns adaptive token-wise switching behaviors while maintaining stable training dynamics. Our code is available at https://github.com/NKU-LITI/TARPO-master.

</details>


### [39] [Analysis of the Neglect-Zero Effect in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05864)
*Jin Tanaka,Daiki Matsuoka,Ryoma Kumon,Hitomi Yanaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探究LLM是否具备人类认知中的“零模型忽略效应”，通过结构启动范式比较LLM与人类在涉及零模型与不涉及零模型的推理任务中的行为差异，发现LLM可能不具备该效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在认知过程中存在“零模型忽略效应”，即倾向于忽略使命题在空集条件下为真 vacuously true 的配置。研究旨在检验LLM的语言处理是否与人类认知过程相似，特别是这种认知偏差是否存在于LLM中。

Method: 采用基于结构启动的结构化范式，设计前句作为启动项以强制LLM考虑零模型，后句为目标句，通过比较涉及零模型与不涉及零模型的推理任务中LLM的行为表现，分析其是否表现出与人类相似的零模型忽略效应。

Result: 实验结果表明，在所分析的LLM中，零模型忽略效应并未出现；即LLM在处理涉及零模型的推理时，并未表现出人类倾向于忽略零模型的倾向。

Conclusion: 这一发现暗示LLM的语言处理机制与人类认知在零模型处理方面存在差异，可能反映了两者在语义推理上的不同特点。研究代码已开源供进一步验证。

Abstract: We investigate the extent to which the language processing of LLMs resembles human cognitive processes, focusing on a human cognitive bias called the $\textit{neglect-zero effect}$. This effect refers to the human tendency to ignore $\textit{zero-models}$, which are configurations that render a proposition vacuously true by virtue of an empty set. We focus on two types of inferences driven by the neglect-zero effect, and examine how LLMs process these inferences by comparing their behavior with that in an inference that does not involve the neglect-zero effect. For this purpose, we employ a paradigm based on $\textit{structural priming}$, where recent exposure to a preceding sentence (the $\textit{prime}$) facilitates the processing of a subsequent sentence (the $\textit{target}$) due to their structural similarity. We prepare primes to force LLMs to consider the zero-model, and analyze whether they also consider it in the target. The results suggest that the neglect-zero effect may not occur in the LLMs analyzed in this study. Our code is available at https://github.com/ynklab/neglect_zero

</details>


### [40] [YouZhi: Towards High-Concurrency Financial LLMs via Adaptive GQA-to-MLA Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05868)
*PSBC LLM Team,Huawei LLM Team,Ruihan Long,Junjie Wu,Tianan Zhang,Duo Zhang,Yaozong Wu,Jinbin Fu,Chang Liu,Zhentao Tang,Wenshuang Yang,Xin Wang,Zhihao Song,Ning Huang,Wenjing Xu,Shuai Zong,Shupei Sun,Sen Wang,Jing Hu,Bin Wang,Xinyu Wang,Junkui Ju,Zequn Ding,Jie Ran,Man Luo,Shixiong Kai,Linkai Hou,Kaichao Liang,Hu Zhao,Yang Zhao,Shucheng Lin,Wei Yu,Chenghan Jiang,Jingjing Ding,Jiahui Zhang,Tian Jin,Yuhang Zhang,Dong Guo,Wei Sun,Jun Xie,Jianwei Li,Lei Cao,Pei Li,Jiabin Li,Jia Yuan,Rui Yuan,Jing Zhu,Mingxuan Yuan,Zhangcheng Lv,Xin Jiang,Xiuhong Fei,Xiaozhe Ren,Yulong Li,Zhipeng Zhang,Hang Wang,Zhaohui Xu,Rui Zhao,Yibo He,Xinzhuang Niu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于华为Ascend生态的金融大模型YouZhi-LLM，通过层自适应GQA-MLA转换框架和Ascend训练管道，显著减少KV缓存开销，提高并发推理能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在金融领域应用广泛，但其高并发部署严重受限于KV缓存内存开销，导致基础设施成本高昂且扩展性受限

Method: 1. 层自适应GQA到MLA转换框架，动态分配每层FreqFold大小以最大化KV缓存压缩；2. 基于Ascend的训练管道，整合通用知识蒸馏与金融领域监督微调

Result: 自适应转换相比均匀基线将困惑度降低最多35%；YouZhi-7B金融基准得分提升12.3%，最大并发量提高2.69倍；YouZhi-14B准确率提升7.0%，并发量提高2.43倍

Conclusion: YouZhi-LLM为成本效益高吞吐量金融推理建立了新范式，证明通过算法优化和硬件协同可显著提升金融LLM部署效率

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) drive significant financial innovations, yet their high-concurrency deployment is severely bottlenecked by KV cache memory overhead, which inflates infrastructure costs and throttles scalability. To address this, we propose YouZhi-LLM, a highly efficient financial LLM empowered by a comprehensive structural transition and training pipeline natively built on the Huawei Ascend ecosystem. At its algorithmic core, YouZhi-LLM features a layer-adaptive GQA-to-MLA transition framework that dynamically assigns per-layer FreqFold sizes, maximizing KV-cache compression while minimizing perplexity degradation. To recover representation capacity and inject domain expertise, the Ascend-based training pipeline seamlessly integrates generalized knowledge distillation with financial-specific supervised fine-tuning. Evaluations demonstrate the superiority of this systematic approach, with the adaptive transition reducing perplexity degradation by up to 35% over uniform baselines. Crucially, when evaluated on Ascend NPUs via vLLM-Ascend, the massive KV-cache reduction translates directly into deployment efficiency. Compared to their respective base models, YouZhi-7B yields a 12.3% improvement in average financial benchmark score alongside a 2.69$\times$ increase in maximum concurrency; similarly, YouZhi-14B achieves a 7.0% accuracy gain and a 2.43$\times$ concurrency boost, establishing a new paradigm for cost-effective, high-throughput financial inference.

</details>


### [41] [Staying with the Uncertainty: Uncertainty-Scaffolding Strategies for Artificial Moral Advisors in LLM-to-LLM Simulated Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05890)
*Salvatore Greco,Hainiu Xu,Jacopo Domenicucci,Yulan He,Sylvie Delacroix*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨大语言模型作为人工智能道德顾问（AMA）在伦理困境对话中如何帮助用户"保持不确定性"，提出三种不确定性模式（视角扩展、张力保留、过程反思）并与三种控制条件（基线、劝说、阿谀奉承）对比，发现所有策略均产生可区分的对话模式且差异在于 engagement 质量而非立场修订程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型越来越多地作为人工智能道德顾问（AMA）部署于各种场景，需要明确其应展现何种对话模式。研究旨在探讨 AMA 如何帮助对话者"保持不确定性"（stay with the uncertainty），即在面对伦理困境时如何有效处理模糊性和不确定性。

Method: 采用用户代理大语言模型与 AMA 进行伦理困境对话，测试三种不确定性模式（视角扩展、张力保留、过程反思）与三种控制条件（基线、劝说、阿谀奉承）的对比效果。研究还考察两种人格提示格式（陈述式、叙事式）。参与者完成对话前后的问卷调查，并分析对话模式、立场变化及 engagement 质量等指标。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 没有单一模型主导模拟用户代理，开放模型通过跨人格分歧对齐人类模糊性，闭源模型通过人格内保留策略实现；(2) 陈述式人格更好地捕捉初始立场多样性，叙事式人格表现出更真实的信念修订；(3) 所有六种 AMA 策略产生可区分的对话模式；(4) 不确定性策略的差异不在于立场修订量，而在于其维持的 engagement 质量。

Conclusion: 不同不确定性策略在 AMA 对话中产生独特的对话模式，其核心区别在于 engagement 质量（而非立场修订量）。研究为设计能够有效支持用户处理伦理不确定性的 AMA 提供了重要见解，表明应关注对话过程中如何维持高质量的 engagement 而非仅关注立场改变的程度。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed as Artificial Moral Advisors (AMA) in a variety of contexts: what kind of conversational patterns should they display? In this paper, we study how AMA can help their interlocutors "stay with the uncertainty". We propose three modes of uncertainty (Perspective-Multiplying, Tension-Preserving, Process-Reflecting) and compare them against three control conditions (Baseline, Persuasive, Sycophantic). A user-agent LLM engages in a dialogue on an ethical dilemma with an AMA following a specific uncertainty strategy, and completes pre- and post-conversation questionnaires. We further examine the effect of two persona prompt formats (Declarative and Narrative). We found that (1) no single model dominates as a simulated user agent, with open models aligning with human ambiguity through between-persona divergence and closed models through within-persona hedging; (2) declarative personas better capture initial stance diversity while narrative personas show more realistic belief revision; (3) all six AMA strategies produce distinguishable conversational patterns; and (4) uncertainty strategies differ not in how much stance revision they produce, but in the quality of engagement they sustain.

</details>


### [42] [Reducing Hallucinations in Complex Question Answering using Simple Graph-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (long version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05901)
*Christopher J. Wedge,Joshua Stutter,Danny Dixon,Jacek Cała*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级图结构工具集来增强RAG系统，显著提升事实正确性、减少幻觉，且token消耗增加不大


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM存在"幻觉"等失败模式；RAG虽能避免幻觉并处理专有信息，但缺乏结构化数据支持；现有方案需要昂贵的模型微调

Method: 设计代理系统，集成多种向量搜索和图查询工具；基于精选的英语维基百科子集构建结构化数据集；在MoNaCo复杂问答基准测试上评估性能

Result: 图工具显著提升事实正确性的精确率和召回率；幻觉答案数量减少50%；细粒度真实性得分在三种评估场景中最高；token使用量仅适度增加

Conclusion: 轻量级图结构工具可有效增强RAG系统性能，在提高准确性和减少幻觉的同时，保持较低的资源消耗

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have fundamentally transformed the landscape of Natural Language Processing. Despite these advances, LLMs and LLM-based systems remain prone to a variety of failure modes. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems have emerged as a common deployment scenario seeking to both avoid the well known risk of the LLM "hallucinating" information, and to enable reasoning and question answering over proprietary information that the LLM did not have access to during training without resorting to expensive model fine-tuning.
  In this work, we explore the idea of using a lightweight graph structure with a relatively simple graph schema, to support the RAG subsystem via a dedicated toolset. We design an agentic system with a variety of vector search and graph query tools operating over a structured dataset based on a curated subset of English Wikipedia articles, and evaluate its performance on questions from MoNaCo, a challenging Wikipedia QA benchmark of complex query answering tasks.
  Our results show that the introduction of graph-based tools can significantly increase the precision and recall of factual correctness, can halve the number of hallucinated answers, and achieves the highest fine-grained truthfulness score among the three evaluated scenarios. All this with a modest increase in token usage.

</details>


### [43] [ACE-SQL: Adaptive Co-Optimization via Empirical Credit Assignment for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05906)
*Xiaobing Chen,Ai Jian,Eryu Guo,Zhiqi Pang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ACE-SQL是一种强化学习框架，通过执行反馈联合优化Text-to-SQL中的模式检索和SQL生成，实现双向自适应优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据库具有大型复杂模式，模式链接是准确SQL生成的关键步骤。现有方法要么依赖全模式生成（在大搜索空间中隐式进行模式链接），要么使用静态金列监督的独立检索器（目标可能对当前生成器策略次优）。

Method: ACE-SQL构建生成器rollout的在线列集池，从与执行正确的rollout最频繁关联的列集中导出自适应on-policy检索目标。这种机制诱导双向适应：检索器适应生成器能正确执行的列集，生成器适应检索器演变的模式选择。

Result: 在约3k个合成Text-to-SQL问题-数据库对上进行RL训练，ACE-SQL在BIRD Dev上达到65.3%的greedy执行准确率，每个查询使用0.93k输出token。

Conclusion: 代码仓库已开源：https://github.com/xbchen1/ACE-SQL

Abstract: Text-to-SQL maps natural language questions to executable SQL queries. Modern databases often contain large and complex schemas, making schema linking a critical step for accurate SQL generation. Existing methods either rely on full-schema generation, which leaves schema linking implicit within a large search space, or use a separate retriever trained with static gold-column supervision, whose targets may be suboptimal for the current generator policy. To address this issue, we propose Adaptive Co-optimization via Empirical Credit Assignment for Text-to-SQL (ACE-SQL), a reinforcement learning (RL) framework that jointly optimizes schema retrieval and SQL generation under execution feedback. ACE-SQL constructs an online column-set pool from generator rollouts and derives adaptive on-policy retrieval targets from the column set most frequently associated with execution-correct rollouts. This induces bidirectional adaptation, where the retriever adapts toward column sets that the generator can execute correctly, while the generator adapts to the retriever's evolving schema selections under execution feedback. With approximately 3k synthetic Text-to-SQL question-database pairs for RL training, ACE-SQL achieves 65.3% greedy execution accuracy on BIRD Dev while using 0.93k output tokens per query. The repository is available at https://github.com/xbchen1/ACE-SQL.

</details>


### [44] [Better Literary Translation: A Multi-Aspect Data Generation and LLM Training Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05924)
*Zhihao Lin,Ziqi Zhu,Hao Huang,Guanghui Wang,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多方面迭代细化框架，利用专用LLM翻译器生成高质量翻译参考和偏好数据，通过SFT和强化学习训练LitMT-8B/14B模型，在MetaphorTrans基准上达到67.25/69.07 CEA100，与Claude Sonnet 4.5相当，且在GRPO上比DPO表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文学翻译面临独特挑战：高质量标注数据稀缺，需要在表达流畅性与文学效果间取得平衡。

Method: 提出多方面迭代细化框架，使用专用LLM翻译器（每个针对不同质量维度）生成高质量翻译参考和偏好数据；采用两阶段训练：监督微调（SFT）和强化学习。

Result: 生成的参考数据在SFT上比原始ground truth高8.65 CEA100点；RL中DPO导致性能下降，而GRPO（结合显式奖励模型）额外提升1.51点；归因于两阶段训练稳定性和GRPO的在线探索能力。LitMT-8B/14B在MetaphorTrans基准上分别达到67.25和69.07 CEA100，与Claude Sonnet 4.5（68.43）相当，且在O. Henry等跨领域文学作品上表现出色。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了文学翻译数据稀缺和表达流畅性与文学效果平衡的挑战；GRPO优于DPO，两阶段训练提供稳定性；生成的LitMT模型在文学翻译任务上具有竞争力的性能和良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Literary translation poses unique challenges due to the scarcity of high-quality annotated data and the need to balance expression fluency with literary effect. We present a multi-aspect iterative refinement framework that generates high-quality translation references and preference data through specialized LLM translators, each targeting a distinct quality dimension. We leverage the generated data for supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Experiments show that our generated references outperform the original ground truth for SFT by 8.65 CEA100 points. For reinforcement learning, we find that DPO leads to performance degradation in this setting, while leveraging an explicit reward model for GRPO yields an additional 1.51 point improvement. We attribute this to the stability of two-stage training and GRPO's online exploration capability. Our resulting models, LitMT-8B and LitMT-14B, achieve 67.25 and 69.07 CEA100 respectively on the MetaphorTrans English-to-Chinese literary translation benchmark, competitive with Claude Sonnet 4.5 at 68.43, and demonstrate strong generalization to out-of-domain literary work (i.e., O. Henry).

</details>


### [45] [Beyond Alignment: Value Diversity as a Collective Property in Multicultural Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05985)
*Shaoyang Xu,Jingshen Zhang,Long P. Hoang,Jinyuan Li,Wenxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"价值多样性"作为多文化多智能体系统的系统级评估指标，发现当前LLM-based社会存在持续的同质化趋势，混合基础模型可部分缓解但无法完全消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文化评估侧重价值对齐（单智能体与目标文化的匹配），无法揭示整个系统是否保留了其代表的文化多样性，需要系统级评估方法。

Method: 提出"价值多样性"作为系统级评估轴，通过不同文化条件下智能体在共享价值调查上的响应之间的不相似性来定义。使用世界价值观调查评估19种文化和18个基础模型，在广泛系统配置下进行测试，包括社交互动和参与式预算案例研究。

Result: 1) 多样性与对齐 largely 不相关，两者捕捉互补属性；2) 当前多文化多智能体系统在价值多样性方面远低于人类社会；3) 混合基础模型系统缩小差距但未完全消除，差距在文化和智能体规模上持续存在；4) 社交互动通过驱动智能体趋向共识进一步侵蚀多样性；5) 参与式预算案例显示同质化缩小集体决策范围。

Conclusion: 价值多样性是多文化多智能体系统的独特评估轴，当前基于LLM的社会表现出持续的同质化倾向，需要在系统设计和评估中重视多样性维护。

Abstract: Multicultural multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed in globally diverse settings, where different agents are grounded in different cultural backgrounds. Existing cultural evaluation focuses on value alignment: how closely a single agent matches a target culture. Yet alignment is a per-agent property and cannot reveal whether a system, taken as a whole, preserves the cultural plurality it is meant to represent. We propose value diversity as a system-level evaluation axis for multicultural agent systems, defined through the dissimilarity between culturally conditioned agents' responses on a shared value survey. Using the World Values Survey, we evaluate 19 cultures and 18 backbone models across a wide range of system configurations. We find that diversity is largely uncorrelated with alignment, indicating that the two capture complementary system properties, and that current multicultural agent systems fall substantially below human societies in value diversity. Mixed-backbone systems narrow this gap but do not close it, and the gap persists across culture compositions and agent scales. Social interaction further erodes diversity by driving agents toward consensus, and a participatory budgeting case study shows that this homogenization narrows the breadth of collective decision-making. Together, our results establish value diversity as a distinct evaluation axis for multicultural multi-agent systems and reveal a persistent homogenization tendency in current LLM-based societies. Our code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/iNLP-Lab/MultiAgent-Diversity.

</details>


### [46] [The Generator-Eraser Paradox: Community Guidelines for Responsible LLM-Assisted Dialect Resource Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06004)
*Wajdi Zaghouani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文探讨了大语言模型在方言资源开发中的双重作用：既能加速方言资源的建设，也可能导致方言同质化和文化侵蚀。通过整合变体社会语言学和语料库语言学，作者提出了"生成器-抹除悖论"理论框架，并推导出12条社区指导原则，最后以阿拉伯语方言为例进行案例分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方言资源处于科学描述、文化保护和计算基础设施的交叉点。大语言模型为方言资源开发提供了强大的检索支持、语料导航、元数据丰富和标注工作流支持等能力。然而，同样的系统也带来重大风险：可能通过优先考虑声望变体、同质化正字法、以及减少语言多样性的合成反馈循环导致方言抹除。这些风险在双言现象、书面标准有限或边缘化说话者社区的语言变体中尤为突出。

Method: 1. 理论框架构建：整合变体社会语言学和语料库语言学，形式化"生成器-抹除悖论"作为理解LLM辅助方言工作的双重性质的理论框架；2. 指导原则推导：基于理论框架推导出12条社区指导原则，将理论转化为方言资源创建和文档记录的可实施设计要求；3. 案例研究：以阿拉伯语方言为深度案例，包括广泛使用的资源结构化比较，展示这些指导原则如何解决语言特定挑战（双言现象、正字法可变性、社区治理）。

Result: 推导出12条可操作的社区指导原则，用于指导方言资源创建和文档记录；提供了阿拉伯语方言的深度案例研究，包括广泛使用的资源结构化比较；建立了LLM辅助方言工作的概念框架和实施要求。案例研究展示了这些指导原则如何解决阿拉伯语方言特有的挑战，如双言现象、正字法可变性和社区治理问题。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是概念性和操作性的而非实验性的，旨在使方言社区和资源构建者能够在不牺牲真实性、变体性或主权的条件下采用大语言模型。通过提供理论框架和可实施的指导原则，为跨语言的方言资源建设者提供了一套系统化的方法，以平衡技术赋能与方言文化保护之间的张力。

Abstract: Dialect resources occupy a unique position at the intersection of scientific description, cultural preservation, and computational infrastructure. Large language models offer powerful capabilities for accelerating dialect resource development through retrieval-grounded drafting, corpus navigation, metadata enrichment, and annotation workflow support. However, the same systems pose substantial risks: they can contribute to dialect erasure by privileging prestige varieties, homogenizing orthography, and enabling synthetic feedback loops that reduce linguistic diversity over time. These risks are particularly acute for language varieties characterized by diglossia, limited written standardization, or marginalized speaker communities. This paper makes three contributions. First, we integrate insights from variationist sociolinguistics and corpus linguistics to formalize the generator-eraser paradox as a theoretical framework for understanding the dual nature of LLM-assisted dialect work. Second, we derive 12 community guidelines that operationalize this framework into implementable design requirements for dialect resource creation and documentation. Third, we provide an in-depth case study of Arabic dialects, including a structured comparison of widely used resources, to demonstrate how these guidelines address language-specific challenges including diglossia, orthographic variability, and community governance. The contribution is conceptual and operational rather than experimental, with the goal of enabling dialect communities and resource builders across languages to adopt LLMs without sacrificing authenticity, variation, or sovereignty.

</details>


### [47] [Contextualized Prompting For Stance Detection On Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06022)
*Tilman Beck,Shakib Yazdani,Simon Kruschinski,Marcus Maurer,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在零样本提示的立场检测任务中融入不同类型上下文信息（真实世界、衍生和LLM生成）的影响，发现LLM生成的目标描述能持续提升性能，而其他用户元数据效果不一，同一用户的其它推文反而可能因噪声影响性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体立场检测面临短文本、噪声和上下文依赖的挑战。现有大语言模型（LLM）在零样本任务中缺乏上下文信息，限制了其对歧义帖子的理解能力，因此需要系统研究如何有效利用各种上下文特征提升零样本立场检测性能。

Method: 系统评估四种基准数据集（含新增高质量德语推特立场数据集），在多种LLM上测试将真实世界（用户简介）、衍生（政党等）和LLM生成（目标描述）的上下文特征融入零样本提示的效果。通过定性与定量分析，发现LLM在区分任务相关与无关上下文时存在困难。

Result: 在多个LLM上，整合上下文信息可提升性能但需特定条件。LLM生成的目标描述 consistently 增强准确率；其他用户元数据效果不一甚至有害；同一用户的其它推文因输入噪声反而损害性能。LLM难以区分任务有用信息与无关上下文。

Conclusion: 上下文提示在噪声现实环境中既有前景又面临挑战，需谨慎设计上下文特征以避免引入噪声。研究发布代码和数据集以促进进一步探索。

Abstract: Stance detection on social media is challenging due to short, noisy, and context-dependent language. While large language models (LLMs) show zero-shot generalization, they are typically prompted without contextual information, which limits their ability to interpret ambiguous posts. In this work, we systematically investigate the impact of incorporating real-world (e.g., user biographies), derived (e.g., political party), and LLM-generated (e.g., target descriptions) contextual features into zero-shot prompting for stance detection on Twitter. Our evaluation spans four benchmark datasets, including a new high-quality German Twitter stance dataset. Across multiple LLMs, we find that integrating contextual information improves performance, but only under specific conditions. LLM-generated target descriptions consistently enhance accuracy, while other user metadata has mixed or even detrimental effects. Notably, we show that the inclusion of other tweets by the same user, often beneficial in supervised learning, can impair performance due to input noise. Our qualitative analysis reveals that LLMs struggle to distinguish task-specific useful information from irrelevant context. Our findings highlight both the promise and challenges of prompting with context information in noisy real-world settings. We publish code and data at this \href{https://github.com/tilmanbeck/stance-context-twitter}{page}.

</details>


### [48] [EGTR-Review: Efficient Evidence-Grounded Scientific Peer Review Generation via Multi-Agent Teacher Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06025)
*Xinpeng Qiu,Wang Yihu,Zhifeng Liu,Xiaochen Wang,Jimin Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EGTR-Review提出了一种基于多代理教师蒸馏的证据支撑可追溯评审生成框架，通过证据加权目标函数和任务前驱多任务学习，在降低推理成本的同时显著提升了评审生成的质量、事实依据和可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的评审方法存在两个主要问题：一是生成的评论过于泛化，缺乏充分证据支持和弱源可追溯性；二是复杂的多代理系统推理成本过高。为了解决这些挑战，需要设计一种既能保证评审质量又能降低推理开销的框架。

Method: EGTR-Review框架包含两个核心阶段：1) 构建多代理教师模型，执行结构感知的论文分解、关键要素提取、外部学术证据检索、证据状态标注、验证推理和评审合成；2) 通过任务前驱驱动的多任务学习，将中间推理轨迹和最终评审评论蒸馏到轻量级学生模型中，并采用证据加权目标函数减少弱/缺失/不可验证监督的影响。

Result: 在公开评审数据集上的实验表明，EGTR-Review(学生模型)在自动评估指标、LLM-as-Judge评估和人工评估中均优于强基线（提示工程、微调、结构化/代理式方法），同时保持了强事实依据和源可追溯性，且显著降低了token消耗和推理时间。

Conclusion: EGTR-Review成功实现了高质量、证据支撑且可追溯的评审生成，同时通过蒸馏技术将推理成本显著降低，为减轻评审负担和提供及时反馈提供了一种高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Scientific peer review generation has attracted increasing attention for reducing reviewing burdens and providing timely feedback. However, existing Large Language Model (LLM)-based methods often produce generic comments with insufficient evidence support and weak source traceability, while complex multi-agent systems incur high inference costs. To address these challenges, we propose EGTR-Review, an Evidence-Grounded and Traceable Review Generation framework via Multi-Agent Teacher Distillation. EGTR-Review first constructs a multi-agent teacher that performs structure-aware paper decomposition, key-element extraction, external scholarly evidence retrieval, evidence-state labeling, verification reasoning, and review synthesis. It then distills both intermediate reasoning trajectories and final review comments into a lightweight student model through task-prefix-driven multi-task learning. An evidence-weighted objective further reduces the influence of weak, missing, or non-verifiable supervision. Experiments on public peer-review datasets show that EGTR-Review (Student) outperforms strong prompt-based, fine-tuned, and structured/agentic baselines across automatic metrics, LLM-as-Judge evaluation, and human evaluation, while maintaining strong factual grounding and source traceability with substantially lower token consumption and inference time. Our code, prompts, configurations, and sample data are available on GitHub.

</details>


### [49] [NAVIRA: Decoupled Stochastic Remasking for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06031)
*Andrey Fomenko,Maksim Kryzhanovskiy,Svetlana Glazyrina,Roman Ischenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出NAVIRA，通过解耦检测与重掩码，并使用温度控制的随机重掩码策略，提升掩码扩散语言模型的生成质量与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型并行去掩码速度快，但早期局部依赖错误会污染上下文。PRISM虽然学习质量分数并重掩码不可靠token，但其推理规则耦合，导致错误token仍参与条件生成，影响最终生成质量。

Method: 提出NAVIRA，将检测低质量token与计算替换logits解耦为两次前向传播。第一次前向传播给token打分，选定token被随机重掩码；第二次前向传播基于清理后的上下文重新生成。采用温度控制的随机重掩码策略，减少重复修正同一位置并平衡流畅度与多样性。

Result: 在170M掩码扩散语言模型上实验，解耦提高了文本流畅度；定时随机重掩码保持了熵，且在相同前向传播预算下获得更强的LLM-judge评分。

Conclusion: 重掩码策略（而非仅依赖学习到的质量信号）是确保掩码扩散语言模型可靠生成的关键因素。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models generate text by iteratively unmasking many tokens in parallel, but this speed comes with a correction problem: tokens generated in the same step are predicted from marginal distributions, and early local dependency errors can later contaminate the context. PRISM addresses this by learning token-level quality scores and remasking unreliable tokens, but its inference rule is coupled: the same forward pass both detects low-quality tokens and computes logits for their replacements, so the erroneous tokens still condition regeneration. We propose NAVIRA, an inference-time decoding policy that separates these two operations and samples remasking positions stochastically. A first forward pass scores tokens; selected tokens are masked; a second forward pass regenerates from the cleaned context. Temperature-controlled remasking reduces repeated correction of the same positions and balances fluency against diversity. In controlled experiments with a 170M masked diffusion language model, decoupling improves fluency, while scheduled stochastic remasking preserves entropy and achieves stronger LLM-judge scores under larger forward-pass budgets. These results show that remasking policy, not only the learned quality signal, is central to reliable masked-diffusion text generation.

</details>


### [50] [IA-RAG: Interval-Algebra-Driven Temporal Reasoning for Dynamic Knowledge Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06044)
*Xiaoman Wang,Yaoze Zhang,Wenzhuo Fan,Hongwei Zhang,Ding Wang,Guohang Yan,Song Mao,Botian Shi,Yunshi Lan,Pinlong Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出IA-RAG，一种分层时序RAG框架，通过区间事件单元(IEU)和Allen区间代数建模知识，引入子图时间紧致机制处理模糊时间边界，在时序问答基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG和图RAG框架将知识视为静态，或仅使用粗粒度时间戳/元数据关联时间，无法捕获丰富的时序结构（如持续时间、重叠、包含关系），限制了模型处理复杂时序推理任务的能力。

Method: 1) 将事实表示为区间事件单元(IEU)并组织成分层主题森林；2) 用Allen区间代数 governing 时序依赖；3) 引入子图时间紧致机制，通过连接事件子图中的逻辑约束细化模糊区间；4) 支持区间代数引导的隐式时序语义检索。

Result: 在TimeQA、TempReason、ComplexTR等时序问答基准测试中，IA-RAG表现出强大的时序检索与推理性能，尤其在复杂组合时序推理任务上优势明显。

Conclusion: IA-RAG有效填补了时序RAG的空白，通过形式化时序约束和区间代数实现了对知识时序结构的精细建模，提升了LLM在时序问答任务中的表现。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has shown strong effectiveness in grounding Large Language Models (LLMs) with external knowledge. However, existing RAG and Graph RAG frameworks largely treat knowledge as static or associate time with coarse-grained timestamps or metadata, failing to capture rich temporal structures such as duration, overlap, and containment. We propose IA-RAG, a hierarchical temporal RAG framework that models knowledge as time intervals and performs retrieval under formal temporal constraints. IA-RAG represents facts as Interval Event Units (IEUs) and organizes them into a hierarchical Thematic Forest, where temporal dependencies are governed by Allen's Interval Algebra. To handle incomplete or uncertain temporal boundaries, IA-RAG further introduces a Sub-graph Time Tightening mechanism that refines fuzzy intervals through logical constraints within connected event subgraphs. In addition, IA-RAG supports implicit temporal semantic retrieval through interval-algebra-guided traversal. Experiments on multiple temporal question answering benchmarks, including TimeQA, TempReason, and ComplexTR, demonstrate that IA-RAG achieves strong temporal retrieval and reasoning performance, particularly on complex compositional temporal reasoning tasks. Our code is released at https://github.com/xiaoAugenstern/LogicalRAG_TemporalQA.

</details>


### [51] [LatentSkill: From In-Context Textual Skills to In-Weight Latent Skills for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06087)
*Aofan Yu,Chenyu Zhou,Tianyi Xu,Zihan Guo,Rong Shan,Zhihui Fu,Jun Wang,Weiwen Liu,Yong Yu,Weinan Zhang,Jianghao Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LatentSkill框架，通过预训练超网络将文本技能转换为LoRA适配器，实现技能知识的权重空间存储


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent系统通过文本编码可复用任务流程，但每次注入提示词会导致大量上下文开销，且技能内容以明文形式暴露

Method: 利用预训练超网络将文本技能转换为可插拔的LoRA适配器，将技能知识存储在权重空间而非上下文空间

Result: 在ALFWorld和Search-QA任务上，LatentSkill在显著减少前缀token使用的同时超越上下文技能基线：ALFWorld成功提升21.4/13.4点（减少64.1%前缀token），Search-QA准确率提升3.0点（减少72.2%技能token开销）

Conclusion: 权重空间技能为扩展LLM Agent提供了高效、模块化且更安全的底层机制

Abstract: Agent systems increasingly use textual skills to encode reusable task procedures, but injecting these skills into the prompt at every step incurs substantial context overhead and exposes skill content as plaintext. We present LatentSkill, a framework that converts textual skills into plug-and-play LoRA adapters through a pretrained hypernetwork. LatentSkill stores skill knowledge in weight space rather than context space, removing per-step skill tokens while preserving modular loading, scaling, and composition. On ALFWorld and Search-QA, LatentSkill outperforms the corresponding in-context skill baseline while using substantially fewer prefill tokens: it improves ALFWorld success by 21.4 and 13.4 points on the seen and unseen splits with 64.1% fewer prefill tokens, and improves Search-QA exact match by 3.0 points with 72.2% lower skill-token overhead. Further analysis shows that generated skill LoRAs form a structured semantic geometry, can be precisely controlled via the LoRA scaling coefficient, and can be composed through parameter-space arithmetic when skill components are aligned. These findings suggest that weight-space skills provide an efficient, modular, and less exposed substrate for extending LLM agents.

</details>


### [52] [IR3DE: A Linear Router for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06098)
*Eros Fanì,Oğuzhan Ersoy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Ridge回归的IR3DE路由器，用于为领域专家模型提供快速、低成本的路由决策，支持动态添加/移除专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着领域专家LLM的出现，需要路由器来选择合适的模型处理不同任务。现有方法要么在弱到强的通用LLM上优化成本，要么需要大量训练来支持领域专家路由。

Method: IR3DE是一种基于Ridge回归的线性路由器，为每个提示提供快速且便宜的路由决策，支持动态添加或移除领域专家而无需从头重新训练路由器。

Result: 在两个CLM设置（通用领域预测）中表现与基线相当，在推理设置中超越基线，归一化性能达到98.4%。支持动态专家管理，无需重新训练。

Conclusion: IR3DE是一个简单有效的线性路由器，在推理任务中表现优异，为LLM管理提供了灵活性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Foundational Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate proficiency on a wide range of general tasks, and achieve remarkable results on various specialized tasks via domain-expert LLMs. With the ever-growing list of available LLMs, inference routers are being proposed to select the most appropriate LLM for each prompt. However, existing routing methods either optimize cost across weak-to-strong generalist LLMs or require substantial training to support domain-expertise routing. In this paper, we propose IR3DE, a Ridge Regression-based Router for Domain Experts that provides cheap and fast routing decisions for each prompt. We evaluate IR3DE in two Causal Language Modeling (CLM) settings where the tasks are next-token prediction for all domains, and one reasoning setting where each domain has its own distinct reasoning task. Despite being a linear router, IR3DE achieves performance comparable to the other baselines in both CLM settings, and surpassing them in the reasoning setting, with a normalized performance of 98.4%. Moreover, IR3DE enables the addition or removal of new domain experts without requiring the router to be retrained from scratch, allowing a dynamic set of LLMs to be served with minimal disruption to the router itself. Our code is available at: github.com/gensyn-ai/IR3DE.

</details>


### [53] [The Tell-Tale Norm: $\ell_2$ Magnitude as a Signal for Reasoning Dynamics in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06188)
*Jinyang Zhang,Hongxin Ding,Yue Fang,Weibin Liao,Muyang Ye,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM隐藏状态的L2范数可作为推理强度的内在信号，并提出三种基于L2范数的测试时扩展技术，无需额外训练即可显著提升模型推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作虽致力于理解LLM推理，但缺乏一种原则性的、模型内在的信号来捕捉层级推理动态。研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用稀疏自编码器(SAE)作为诊断探针，发现推理特征在深层显著激活；建立推理强度与潜在几何的正式联系；理论证明L2范数界定了SAE推理特征的激活强度；通过相关性分析和因果干预验证L2范数作为推理指标的有效性；提出三种测试时扩展技术：自适应层级推理递归、内生推理状态引导和L2引导响应选择。

Result: L2范数与关键推理步骤高度相关；基于L2范数的技术在多种模型架构和基准测试上显著提升推理性能，且无需额外训练或数据。

Conclusion: L2范数提供了感知和控制LLM潜在推理动态的原则性且简单的视角，为推理增强提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Recent work has sought to understand Large Language Models (LLMs) reasoning, yet a principled, model-intrinsic signal that captures its layer-wise reasoning dynamics remains underexplored. We bridge this gap by demonstrating that the l2 norm of hidden states serves as an endogenous signal of the model's reasoning intensity. Using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) as a diagnostic probe, we observe that LLMs' internal reasoning is marked by a sharp increase in reasoning feature activations concentrated in late layers. Motivated by this pattern, we establish a formal link between reasoning intensity and the model's latent geometry and theoretically prove that the l2 norm of hidden states bounds the activation strength of SAE reasoning features. Empirical correlation analysis and causal interventions further validate the l2 norm as a faithful indicator, where heightened norms consistently correspond to critical reasoning steps. We then introduce three test-time scaling techniques guided by l2 norms: (i) Adaptive Layer-wise Reasoning Recursion, (ii) Endogenous Reasoning State Steering, and (iii) l2-guided Response Selection, which requires no additional training or data and is compatible with advanced inference engines. Experiments across model architectures and benchmarks show that l2-norm-based techniques significantly improve reasoning performance, offering a principled yet simple lens to perceive and control LLM latent reasoning dynamics. Our code is available at https://github.com/zjy1298/The-Tell-Tale-Norm.

</details>


### [54] [Improving Answer Extraction in Context-based Question Answering Systems Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06197)
*Hafez Abdelghaffar,Ahmed Alansary,Ali Hamdi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的问答系统，通过在SQuAD1.1数据集上对预训练模型进行微调，以提升上下文理解和答案提取能力。实验结果表明，微调后的Roberta-base模型在ROUGE-L、BLEU和BERTScore等指标上取得了优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有问答系统在处理复杂或模糊查询时，仍面临上下文理解、答案一致性和跨领域泛化等挑战，往往产生不相关或不精确的响应。

Method: 对预训练语言模型进行微调，使用Stanford Question Answering Dataset (SQuAD1.1)作为训练和评估数据集，具体采用Roberta-base模型。

Result: 微调后的Roberta-base模型取得ROUGE-L为86.84%、BLEU为28.24%、BERTScore为95.38%，显示出高准确性和答案相关性。

Conclusion: 针对性微调显著提升了问答系统的可靠性和精确度，验证了该方法在基于上下文的问答任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) systems have achieved notable progress with the advent of large language models (LLMs). However, they still face challenges in accurately extracting and generating precise answers from given contexts, particularly when dealing with complex or ambiguous queries. Existing approaches often struggle with contextual understanding, answer consistency, and generalization across diverse domains. In this work, we propose a question answering system based on large language models, where the input consists of a textual context and a corresponding question, and the output is a concise and accurate answer. The motivation behind this research lies in addressing the limitations of current QA systems, particularly their tendency to produce irrelevant or imprecise responses despite having access to the correct context. Our methodology involves fine-tuning a pre-trained LLM on a benchmark QA dataset to improve its contextual comprehension and answer extraction capabilities. Specifically, we utilize the Stanford Question Answering Dataset (SQuAD1.1), which provides high-quality context-question-answer triplets for supervised training and evaluation. Experimental results show that the fine-tuned Roberta-base model achieves the highest performance, attaining a ROUGE-L score of 86.84%, a BLEU score of 28.24%, and a BERTScore of 95.38%. These results indicate strong accuracy and answer relevance, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed approach for context-based question answering tasks. Furthermore, the findings confirm that targeted fine-tuning substantially improves the reliability and precision of QA systems.

</details>


### [55] [Dense Contexts Are Hard Contexts: Lexical Density Limits Effective Context in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06203)
*Giovanni Dettori,Matteo Boffa,Danilo Giordano,Idilio Drago,Marco Mellia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究词汇密度对大语言模型长上下文性能的影响，发现词汇密度是影响有效上下文容量的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输入长度和相关信息位置被广泛认为是导致大语言模型长上下文性能下降的主要原因，但本研究引入第三个因素——词汇密度。

Method: 使用三个“寻针”风格基准测试，保持长度（约12k tokens）和针位置不变，仅增加信息密度；在9B-685B参数量的开放权重大语言模型上测试；在保持其他属性不变的情况下控制每个基准测试中的密度。

Result: 在高密度基准测试中出现性能急剧下降；在稀疏上下文中表现接近完美的模型在密度更高的测试中检索得分降至60%以下；降低密度通常能恢复性能。

Conclusion: 有效上下文容量是词汇密度的函数，这对实际运行在紧凑、信息丰富输入上的大语言模型系统具有直接影响。

Abstract: Input length and the position of relevant information are widely cited as the primary causes of degraded LLM long-context performance. Here, we study lexical density -- the rate at which a context introduces distinct information -- as a third, largely overlooked factor that systematically reduces the effective context window of LLMs. We quantify the impact of lexical density on open-weight LLMs (9B-685B) using three "find-the-needle" style benchmarks with identical length (~12k tokens) and controlled needle position, but increasing density of information. We observe a sharp performance collapse in higher-density benchmarks: models that are near-perfect in sparse contexts drop below 60% retrieval score on denser ones. To rule out task-type confounds, we vary and control the density within each benchmark while keeping all other properties unchanged. Reducing density generally restores performance, especially in the high-density regimes where degradation appears. These results show that effective context capacity is a function of lexical density, with direct implications for real-world LLM systems operating on compact, information-rich inputs.

</details>


### [56] [FOXGLOVE: Understanding Goal-Oriented and Anchored Writing Feedback from Experts and LLMs on Argumentative Essays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06271)
*Yijun Liu,Yifan Song,John Gallagher,Sarah Sterman,Tal August*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FOXGLOVE数据集包含2,340条来自人类教师和四个前沿大语言模型的写作反馈评论，用于系统比较LLM与专家反馈在写作修改核心维度上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）越来越多地用于生成写作反馈，但目前尚无系统比较LLM与专家反馈在写作研究识别的核心修改维度（目标导向、针对具体句子的定位、优先级）上的研究。

Method: FOXGLOVE数据集包含696条由训练有素的写作教师对69篇十二年级议论文撰写的反馈评论，以及1,644条由四个前沿LLM在统一协议下生成的评论，共计2,340条。对其中一部分教师和LLM评论进行了专家质量评分。

Result: 教师和LLM在目标分布和文章位置上的反馈分布相似，但在具体提供反馈的句子选择上存在差异。模型倾向于撰写更复杂的反馈并使用更少的提问。由教师评级的LLM反馈在大多数质量维度上得分更高，但这一优势主要归因于评论长度更长。

Conclusion: FOXGLOVE能够系统比较人类与LLM反馈在哪些方面一致、哪些方面分歧、哪些方面存在差异，为改进LLM写作反馈系统提供基础。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate writing feedback, there remains no systematic comparison of LLM and expert feedback on the dimensions that writing research identifies as central to revision: goal-orientation, anchoring to specific sentences, and prioritization. We introduce FOXGLOVE, a dataset of 696 feedback comments written by trained writing instructors on 69 twelfth-grade argumentative essays, paired with 1,644 comments generated from four frontier LLMs under a shared protocol, totaling 2,340 comments. We provide expert quality ratings on a subset of both instructor and LLM comments. We find that instructors and LLMs distribute feedback similarly across goals and essay positions, yet instructors and models diverge on the specific sentences on which to provide feedback. Additionally, we find that models tend to write more complex feedback and use fewer questions than instructors. LLM feedback also receives higher ratings on most dimensions of quality, as rated by instructors, but much of this advantage appears to be attributable to lengthier comments. FOXGLOVE enables systematic comparison of where human and LLM feedback align, diverge, and differ.

</details>


### [57] [EDIT: Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training for Rule-Faithful LLM Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06350)
*Zhihao Wu,Linhai Zhang,Taiyi Wang,Runcong Zhao,Peter Andrews,Cesare Aloisi,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出证据诊断干预训练（EDIT）框架，通过两阶段训练提升LLM评分的 rubric 依赖性：第一阶段（EDIT-SFT）利用内部模型信号定位并修正有问题的推理步骤；第二阶段（EDIT-RL）通过信念引导的奖励塑造进行校准。在两个多学科评分基准上的实验表明，EDIT 在跨域任务中持续优于监督微调和强化学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的 rubric 评分不仅需要准确的分数预测，还需要评分过程基于评分标准和学生答案的证据。现有针对自包含推理任务（如数学推理）的信用分配和干预方法在此场景下失效，因为它们无法识别评分推理出错的位置，也无法追踪模型在推理过程中对最终分数信念的变化。

Method: EDIT 包含两个阶段：1) EDIT-SFT：利用内部模型信号（最终分数的后验信念和输入 grounded 分数）定位有问题的推理步骤，并借助 rubric 检查表仅修正这些局部步骤；2) EDIT-RL：通过信念引导的奖励塑造对评分器进行校准，在惩罚有害信念漂移的同时允许有益探索。

Result: 在两个真实世界多学科评分基准上，EDIT 在跨域和跨领域划分中持续优于强监督微调和强化学习基线；消融研究证实内部状态诊断驱动了这些性能提升。

Conclusion: 结合内部状态诊断与针对性干预及奖励塑造的 EDIT 框架，能有效提升 LLM 评分器的 rubric 依赖性，实现更可靠和可解释的评分。

Abstract: Reliable rubric grading requires more than accurate score prediction. Each judgement must be grounded in the mark scheme and evidence from the student answer. Existing credit-assignment and intervention methods, primarily designed for self-contained reasoning tasks such as mathematics reasoning, struggle in this setting because they do not identify where grading reasoning goes wrong or how the model's belief about the final mark changes during reasoning. We propose Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training (EDIT), a two-phase framework for training more rubric-faithful LLM graders. First, EDIT-SFT locates problematic reasoning steps using internal model signals: posterior belief over the final mark and input-grounding scores. It then revises only these local steps with help from a rubric checklist. Second, EDIT-RL calibrates the grader with belief-guided reward shaping, penalising large harmful belief drifts while still allowing helpful exploration. Experiments on two real-world, multi-subject grading benchmarks demonstrate that EDIT consistently outperforms strong supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning baselines on both in-domain and out-of-domain splits, with ablation studies confirming that internal-state diagnostics drive these gains.

</details>


### [58] [CollabSim: A CSCW-Grounded Methodology for Investigating Collaborative Competence of LLM Agents through Controlled Multi-Agent Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06399)
*Jiaju Chen,Bo Sun,Yuxuan Lu,Yun Wang,Dakuo Wang,Bingsheng Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CollabSim框架，用于系统评估多智能体系统（MAS）中智能体的协作能力，通过理论驱动的定义、受控交互条件和内部状态探测，揭示了LLM智能体在协作中的性能模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS评估主要关注任务结果或单个智能体的推理、规划和工具使用能力，但研究表明MAS失败往往源于协作能力不足（如共同基础建立、共享理解维持、个体与集体激励平衡、误解修复），而非个体任务解决能力问题。

Method: CollabSim是一个可配置的模拟框架，结合了理论驱动的协作能力定义、对交互条件的受控操纵，以及动作级智能体内部状态探测。

Result: 在四个LLM上的实验表明，CollabSim能够捕捉条件效应、分离模型性能模式，并揭示智能体设计的任务依赖效应。

Conclusion: CollabSim为系统分析MAS中智能体的协作能力提供了有效工具，弥补了现有评估方法的不足。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models have shown growing promise, with their effectiveness resting on agents' ability to coordinate through text-based channels much as human teams do. Yet recent study suggests that MAS often falter not because agents lack individual task-solving ability, but because they lack collaborative competence: the capacity to establish common ground, maintain shared task understanding, balance individual and collective incentives, and repair misalignment as interaction unfolds. Decades of research in Computer-Supported Cooperative Work have characterized these requirements for human teams coordinating under constrained communication, yet existing MAS evaluations focus mainly on task outcomes or single-agent proficiency in reasoning, planning, and tool use. To enable a systematic analysis of agents' collaborative competence in MAS, we introduce CollabSim, a configurable simulation framework that combines a theory-grounded definition of collaborative capabilities, controlled manipulation of interaction conditions, and action-level probing of agents' internal states. Experiments across four LLMs show that CollabSim can capture condition effects, separate model performance patterns, and reveal task-dependent effects of agent design.

</details>


### [59] [Reinforcement Learning Elicits Contextual Learning of Unseen Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06428)
*Hanxu Hu,Zdeněk Šnajdr,Pinzhen Chen,Jannis Vamvas,Rico Sennrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的翻译框架，通过从丰富的语言上下文中提取和应用相关语言知识，使LLM在未见语言上实现比上下文学习和监督微调更好的翻译效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM低资源语言翻译方法（持续训练、上下文编码）存在过拟合问题且零样本迁移能力有限；作者认为LLM必须掌握利用上下文语言知识的元技能，而非记忆特定语言，才能在规模化翻译极低资源语言。

Method: 提出基于强化学习的未见语言翻译方法，利用表面级翻译指标chrF作为奖励信号，在丰富的语言上下文支持下训练模型提取和应用相关语言信息。

Result: 实验表明，尽管奖励信号轻量，RL训练的模型能有效从上下文中提取相关语言信息，在完全未见语言上的翻译效果优于上下文学习和监督微调；分析显示基于结果的RL可用于语言学习。

Conclusion: 基于结果的强化学习不仅适用于数学和编程等推理任务，还可作为从上下文中学习语言的通用方法，使LLM具备更好的泛化翻译能力。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can translate unseen or low-resource languages by undergoing continued training or even by encoding a grammar book in their context. However, both methods typically overfit specific languages, with limited zero-shot transfer at test time. To translate extremely low-resource languages at scale, we argue that LLMs must acquire the meta-skill of utilizing in-context linguistic knowledge rather than memorizing specific languages. In this paper, we propose a reinforcement learning (RL) approach to unseen language translation given rich linguistic context, using a surface-level translation metric (chrF) as the reward. Empirically, despite the lightweight reward, our RL-trained models effectively extract and apply relevant linguistic information from the provided context, leading to better translations on completely unseen languages than in-context learning or supervised fine-tuning. Our analyses suggest that outcome-based RL can extend beyond conventional reasoning tasks like math and coding to serve as a recipe for language learning from context.

</details>


### [60] [Latent Reasoning with Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06447)
*Guancheng Tu,Xiangjun Fu,Suhao Yu,Yao Tang,Haoqiang Kang,Lianhui Qin,Yizhe Zhang,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NF-CoT是一种基于归一化流的潜推理框架，在保留链式思考优势的同时，通过紧凑的连续状态执行中间计算，在代码生成基准上提升了通过率并大幅降低了推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式链式思考虽然能提升推理能力，但文本形式的推理存在局限：离散的串行token流要求每个推理步骤都必须先被语言化，即使底层更新是语义性的、不确定的或部分形成的。现有潜推理方法往往牺牲了链式思考的关键优势，如原生从左到右生成、概率采样、与KV缓存的兼容性以及可处理的似然估计。

Method: NF-CoT通过归一化流建模连续思维，在LLM骨干网络中实例化TARFlow风格的归一化流，为从显式链式思考蒸馏出的紧凑连续思维定义可处理的概率模型。连续思维位置由NF头生成，文本位置由标准LM头在同一因果流中生成。这种设计提供了潜思维的精确似然值，支持与原始KV缓存兼容的概率从左到右解码，并支持潜推理空间中的直接策略梯度优化。

Result: 在代码生成基准测试中，NF-CoT的通过率优于显式链式思考和先前的潜推理基线，同时大幅减少了中间推理成本。

Conclusion: NF-CoT证明了潜推理可以在保留显式链式思考优势的同时更加高效，在代码生成任务上实现了更优的性能表现和更低的计算开销。

Abstract: Large language models often improve reasoning by generating explicit chain-of-thought (CoT), demonstrating the importance of intermediate computation. However, textual CoT forces this computation through a discrete, serial, and communication-oriented token stream: each reasoning step must be verbalized before the model can proceed, even when the underlying update is semantic, uncertain, or only partially formed. Latent reasoning offers a higher-bandwidth alternative by performing intermediate computation in compact continuous states before committing to text. Yet existing latent-reasoning methods often sacrifice key advantages that make CoT effective in autoregressive language models, including native left-to-right generation, probabilistic sampling, compatibility with KV-cache decoding, and tractable likelihood estimation. We propose NF-CoT, a latent reasoning framework that preserves these advantages by modeling continuous thoughts with normalizing flows. NF-CoT instantiates a TARFlow-style normalizing flow inside the LLM backbone, defining a tractable probability model over compact continuous thoughts distilled from explicit CoT. Continuous-thought positions are generated by an NF head, while text positions are generated by the standard LM head within the same causal stream. This design provides exact likelihoods for latent thoughts, enables probabilistic left-to-right decoding with the original KV cache, and supports direct policy-gradient optimization in the latent reasoning space. On code-generation benchmarks, NF-CoT improves pass rates over explicit-CoT and prior latent-reasoning baselines while substantially reducing intermediate-reasoning cost.

</details>


### [61] [Human Adults and LLMs as Scientists: Who Benefits from Active Exploration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06464)
*Mandana Samiei,Eunice Yiu,Anthony GX-Chen,Dongyan Lin,Jocelyn Shen,Blake A. Richards,Alison Gopnik,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 成年人在被动观察下难以识别联合因果规则，但在主动探索后表现改善，但仍不如析取规则。大语言模型在准确率上接近人类，但探索效率较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果学习文献中存在长期发现：成年人在识别联合因果规则（需多个原因同时存在）时表现较差，而识别析取规则（至少一个原因存在即可）时表现更好。大多数此类发现依赖被动观察范式，学习者无法控制证据生成。研究旨在探究当成年人被赋予主动探索权力时，这种偏差是否仍然存在。

Method: 采用修改版的"blicket detector"任务，让成年参与者主动干预来识别联合或析取规则结构下的因果对象。同时将人类表现与一系列大语言模型在同一设置下进行比较。

Result: 主动探索显著改善了成年人的联合因果推理能力，尽管联合规则仍比析取规则需要更多测试来推断。一些最先进的大语言模型在假设推断准确率上接近人类水平，但它们通常表现出探索效率较低，且具有类似的联合-析取性能差距。

Conclusion: 主动探索可以缓解成年人在联合因果推理中的困难，表明控制证据生成对于因果学习的重要性。大语言模型虽然能达到高准确率，但在探索策略上不如人类高效。

Abstract: A long-standing finding in the causal learning literature is that adults struggle to identify conjunctive causal rules, where an effect requires the simultaneous presence of multiple causes, while performing better in disjunctive settings. However, most demonstrations of this ``conjunctive handicap'' rely on passive observation paradigms with limited evidence, where learners have no control over evidence generation. This paper asks whether this bias persists when adults are granted agency through active exploration. Using a modified ``blicket detector'' task, adult participants freely intervened to identify causal objects under conjunctive or disjunctive rule structures. We show that active exploration substantially improves adults' conjunctive causal reasoning, although conjunctive rules still require more tests to infer than disjunctive rules. We further compare human performance to a range of large language models in the same setting. While some state-of-the-art models approach human-level performance on hypothesis inference accuracy, they often exhibit less efficient exploration strategies and similar conjunctive-disjunctive performance gaps.

</details>


### [62] [You Only Index Once: Cross-Layer Sparse Attention with Shared Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06467)
*Yutao Sun,Yanqi Zhang,Li Dong,Jianyong Wang,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出跨层稀疏注意力（CLSA），在KV共享架构基础上共享KV缓存和路由索引，通过复用单一索引器计算结果，在保持token级稀疏选择性的同时分摊路由开销，实现端到端推理效率与精度的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理在推理密集场景下受解码效率限制，现有稀疏注意力方法存在效率-质量权衡：结构化块稀疏加速强但质量损失明显，token稀疏精度高但路由开销导致端到端加速有限。

Method: 在YOCO等KV共享架构基础上构建跨层稀疏注意力（CLSA），核心思想是跨交叉解码层共享KV缓存和路由索引，单一索引器计算token级top-k选择一次并在各层复用索引，既保留token稀疏的细粒度选择性，又分摊路由开销。

Result: 在短/长上下文基准测试中，CLSA在128K上下文时实现7.6倍解码加速和17.1倍整体吞吐提升，同时改善预填充、KV缓存存储和长上下文解码三大推理瓶颈。

Conclusion: CLSA为长上下文LLM提供了一种同时提升模型质量和推理效率的完整架构解决方案，在保持精度的前提下显著增强解码效率。

Abstract: Long-context inference in modern LLMs is increasingly constrained by decoding efficiency, especially in reasoning-heavy settings where models generate long intermediate chains of thought. Existing sparse attention methods often face a practical efficiency-quality trade-off. Structured block sparse methods typically provide stronger acceleration but incur noticeable quality loss, while token sparse methods are usually more accurate yet deliver limited end-to-end speedup because top-k routing over the full cache remains expensive. In this work, we propose cross-layer sparse attention (CLSA), which is built on top of KV-sharing architectures such as YOCO. The core idea is to share not only the KV cache across cross-decoder layers, but also the routing index. A single indexer computes token-level top-k selection once and reuses the resulting index across layers, thereby preserving the fine-grained selectivity of token sparse attention while amortizing the routing overhead. The resulting architecture improves all major inference bottlenecks jointly, including pre-filling, KV-cache storage, and long-context decoding. Experiments across short-context and long-context benchmarks show that CLSA is both accurate and efficient, achieving up to 7.6x decoding speedup and 17.1x overall throughput improvement at 128K context. These results suggest a more complete architectural solution for long-context LLMs that jointly advances model quality and inference efficiency.

</details>

### [63] [How Far Did They Go? The Persuasive Tactics of Covert LLM Agents in a Discontinued Field Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05256)
*Kokil Jaidka,Saifuddin Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究分析了Reddit r/ChangeMyView版块一个公开的AI生成评论数据集，通过结构化内容分析揭示AI代理在身份表现、权威信号、对齐策略和认知启发式激活方面的修辞架构特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成内容在身份丰富的 deliberative 论坛中的出现，研究旨在探索大型语言模型在没有披露的情况下如何运作，以及如何评估AI系统构建可信度的能力，而不仅仅是检测它们的存在。

Method: 对Reddit版主发布的AI生成评论档案进行结构化内容分析，评估四个维度：身份表现、权威信号、对齐策略、认知启发式激活；并与人类撰写的CMV反驳意见进行对比分析。

Result: 超过三分之二的评论涉及身份定位/采用，几乎全部包含对齐动作和权威声明，大多数触发认知偏差（确认、代表性、可得性）；AI代理在权威使用密度、对抗性对齐、外部引用依赖上均显著区别于人类作者。

Conclusion: AI代理构建了为说服效率而非真实参与服务的修辞架构，导致真实与合成认识地位界限模糊。结果指向需要审计框架来评估AI系统如何构建可信度，而不仅仅是披露要求。

Abstract: This study analyzes a publicly released dataset from a discontinued field experiment on Reddit's r/ChangeMyView. The intervention, conducted by unknown, external researchers and halted following ethical backlash, involved undisclosed AI-generated accounts engaging users in live debate. After public disclosure, Reddit authorized moderators to release an archive of the AI-generated comments, creating a rare opportunity to examine how large language models operated in an identity-rich deliberative forum without disclosure. We conduct a structured content analysis of this corpus, evaluating identity performance, authority signaling, alignment strategies, and activation of cognitive heuristics. Identity targeting or adoption appears in over two-thirds of comments, alignment moves and authority claims in nearly all of them, and cognitive-bias triggers -- particularly confirmation bias, representativeness, and availability -- in the large majority. These patterns co-occur systematically, composing a rhetorical architecture calibrated for persuasive efficiency rather than authentic deliberative participation. Compared against human-authored CMV counter-arguments, the agents inverted the typical distribution on every dimension: denser authority use, more adversarial alignment, and heavier reliance on external citation over experiential grounding. In such environments, distinctions between authentic and synthetic epistemic standing grow increasingly opaque -- an asymmetry that disclosure mandates alone cannot address. The results point toward auditing frameworks capable of assessing how AI systems structure credibility, not merely whether they are present.

</details>


### [64] [What Should Agents Say? Action-state Communication for Efficient Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05304)
*Chen Huang,Yuhao Wu,Wenxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACT通过将原始智能体输出投影为紧凑的行动状态记录，优化了多智能体系统的通信，在保持或提高性能的同时显著减少token使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大型语言模型的多智能体系统（MAS）使用自由形式的自然语言通信，这导致token使用快速增长、消耗共享上下文窗口，最终影响系统性能和推理成本。

Method: 提出PACT（Protocolized Action-state Communication and Transmission），将智能体间通信视为公共状态更新问题，在原始智能体输出进入共享历史之前将其投影为紧凑的行动状态记录。

Result: 在多种MAS拓扑结构中，PACT一致地改善了性能-成本权衡，在显著减少token的同时实现相当的或更强的任务性能。在OpenHands中，PACT在减少10% token的情况下提高了解决率；在SWE-agent中，PACT在保持解决率的同时将输入token减半。

Conclusion: 没有固定的通信策略是普遍最优的，有效的通信策略始终保留下游智能体所需的以行动为中心的信息。PACT验证了将通信视为公共状态更新问题的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models are typically organized around roles, pipelines, and turn schedules, while the content that agents pass to one another is often left as unconstrained natural language. However, this free-form communication can rapidly inflate token usage, consume the shared context window, and ultimately affect both system performance and inference cost. We analyze five common inter-agent communication strategies across two MAS topologies, finding that no fixed strategy is universally optimal. Instead, effective inter-agent messages consistently preserve action-centered information needed by downstream agents. Building on this, we propose the PACT (Protocolized Action-state Communication and Transmission), which treats inter-agent communication as a public state-update problem and projects each raw agent output into a compact action-state record before it enters shared history. Across different MAS topologies, PACT consistently improves the performance-cost trade-off, achieving comparable or stronger task performance with substantially fewer tokens. The gains extend to production coding harnesses: PACT lifts OpenHands' resolve rate at -10% tokens-per-resolved, and is resolve-neutral on SWE-agent while halving input tokens. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/iNLP-Lab/PACT.

</details>


### [65] [Synthetic Contrastive Reasoning for Multi-Table Q&A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05382)
*Ankit Pratap Singh,Xin Su,Phillip Howard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 构建合成对比推理轨迹数据集，使用对比偏好优化（CPO）微调开放权重LLM，在多表问答任务上相比问答监督微调提升9.7%-16.3%，MMQA任务最高提升21个百分点


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多表问答需要模型跨关系表检索相关证据、链接模式并执行组合推理，但现有资源仅提供问题和最终答案，缺乏解释答案推导过程的推理监督，存在监督信号缺失的缺口

Method: 利用异构LLM生成经过验证的正样本轨迹和合理的负样本轨迹，构建合成对比推理轨迹数据集，使用对比偏好优化（CPO）微调Qwen3-14B、Mistral-8B和Llama-3.1-8B等开放权重LLM，进行消融实验和评估

Result: CPO相比问答监督微调在平均绝对提升9.7%-16.3%，MMQA任务最高提升21个百分点；消融实验表明异构生成器增强了对比信号；自动和人工评估显示生成对在忠实度、连贯性和对比性方面表现良好

Conclusion: 通过合成对比推理轨迹进行偏好优化能有效提升多表问答性能，证明了偏好优化方法相比直接问答监督的优越性，为多表QA任务提供了有效的监督信号构建途径

Abstract: Multi-table question answering requires models to retrieve relevant evidence, link schemas, and perform compositional reasoning across relational tables. Existing multi-table Q&A resources typically provide questions and final answers but lack reasoning supervision that explains how answers are derived. To address this gap, we construct a synthetic contrastive reasoning-trace dataset for MMQA by generating validated positive traces and plausible negative traces with heterogeneous LLMs. We then use the resulting preference pairs to fine-tune open-weight LLMs with Contrastive Preference Optimization (CPO). Across Qwen3-14B, Mistral-8B, and Llama-3.1-8B, CPO achieves absolute average improvements over Q&A supervised fine-tuning ranging from 9.7%-16.3%, with gains up to 21 percentage points on MMQA. Ablations show that heterogeneous positive and negative trace generators strengthen the contrastive signal, and automated as well as human evaluations indicate that the generated pairs are largely faithful, coherent, and meaningfully contrastive.

</details>


### [66] [Stability vs. Manipulability: Evaluating Robustness Under Post-Decision Interaction in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05384)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM-as-judge评估在交互中存在决策后可操纵性问题：LLM法官在重复和中立重新评估时稳定，但面对定向挑战时判断可逆转，且这种逆转常伴随低重叠的事后合理化，导致基准评估结果不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-as-judge评估管道假设判断是固定输入的稳定属性，但该假设在交互下不成立。作者旨在研究评估结果在决策后交互中的可操纵性，揭示这一评估失败模式。

Method: 在MT-Bench和AlpacaEval上开展控制实验，通过anti-baseline挑战协议和counterbalanced target-validation协议研究决策后可操纵性，引入Evaluation Robustness Score量化交互鲁棒性。

Result: LLM法官在重复和中立重新评估下高度稳定，但面对定向决策后挑战时变得显著可逆；权威框架尤其不稳定，且逆转常伴随低重叠的合理化；这种逆转会降低与人类偏好的同意度、改变基准排名并产生有害评估变化。

Conclusion: 决策后交互是LLM-as-judge评估的独立失败模式，需要评估协议不仅测量静态一致性，还应衡量在挑战下的鲁棒性。引入ERS量化交互鲁棒性，建议在基准测试中纳入鲁棒性指标以避免评估结果被操纵。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge evaluation is widely used in benchmarking pipelines, where model outputs are compared and ranked using automated evaluators.
