Moreover, MLEvolve also outperforms specialized algorithm discovery methods including AlphaEvolve on mathematical algorithm optimization tasks, demonstrating strong cross-domain generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/InternScience/MLEvolve.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Temporal Preference Concepts and their Functions in a Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05194)
*Ian Rios-Sialer,Shantanu Darveshi,Shuai Jiang,Avigya Paudel,Anastasiia Pronina,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过因果定位和机制可解释性方法，揭示了LLM内部如何编码和处理时间偏好权衡，发现其未来折扣行为与人类不同且不稳定，转向向量可调节时间偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs越来越多地被用于需要权衡短期收益和长期后果的决策，但对其内部如何表示或解决这些权衡知之甚少。

Method: 在蒸馏LLM（Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507）中，通过梯度归因和激活补丁的收敛证据，因果定位时间偏好的底层子图，识别中上层节点。

Result: 1) 时间视野的几何结构编码在残差流中预期的定位层；2) 未干预的LLM对未来折扣的幅度比人类小；3) 这种偏好在不同语境下不稳定；4) 转向向量可以改变时间偏好。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性使我们对如何可靠地控制LLM的规划和推理更接近，支持显式控制而非依赖训练隐式机制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed to make decisions that require trading off near-term gains against long-term consequences, yet little is known about how they internally represent or resolve these tradeoffs. In this work, we causally localize an underlying subgraph for temporal preference in a distilled LLM (Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507), identifying mid-to-upper-layer nodes through converging evidence from gradient-based attribution and activation patching. We find that the geometry of time horizon is encoded in the residual stream at the expected localized layers. A behavioral analysis reveals that unintervened LLMs discount the future several times less steeply than humans, yet this preference is unstable across contexts, motivating explicit control rather than implicit reliance on training. Finally, we find suggestive evidence that steering vectors can shift temporal preference. Our work demonstrates how mechanistic interpretability can bring us closer to reliable control over how LLMs plan and reason

</details>


### [104] [Agentic Monte Carlo: Simulating Reinforcement Learning for Black-Box Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05296)
*Dae Yon Hwang,Raunaq Suri,Valentin Villecroze,Anthony L. Caterini,Jesse C. Cresswell,Noël Vouitsis,Brendan Leigh Ross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Agentic Monte Carlo (AMC)方法，通过将RL等价于贝叶斯推断，在不改变黑盒LLM模型参数的前提下，利用序列蒙特卡洛采样从最优策略轨迹后验分布中采样，实现对黑盒LLM agents的RL风格优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM agents分为开放权重（可RL优化）和黑盒（仅测试时控制）两类。黑盒agent虽由最先进专有LLM支持，但API访问限制参数级优化，导致传统RL方法不可用。需要一种方法在不修改黑盒模型的前提下优化其行为。

Method: 利用RL与贝叶斯推断的等价性，提出Agentic Monte Carlo (AMC)：将黑盒agent视为先验，最优策略定义为轨迹的后验分布；使用序列蒙特卡洛从该后验采样，通过学习价值函数引导agent行为，同时保持底层黑盒模型不变。

Result: 在AgentGym基准的三个多样化环境中验证，AMC显著优于提示基线，且随着测试时计算量增加，性能超越Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)，证明了黑盒LLM agents可进行原则性RL风格优化。

Conclusion: AMC成功实现了对黑盒LLM agents的原则性RL风格优化，无需访问模型参数。该方法证明了在不改变黑盒模型的情况下，可以通过测试时优化提升agent性能，为API访问的LLM agents提供了新的优化途径。

Abstract: LLM agents operate in two distinct regimes: open-weight agents amenable to reinforcement learning (RL) and black-box agents whose behaviour must be controlled purely at test time. Although black-box agents are often backed by state-of-the-art proprietary LLMs, API-only access precludes parameter-level optimization, rendering most RL methods inapplicable. To address this limitation, we turn to a known equivalence between RL and Bayesian inference. We propose Agentic Monte Carlo (AMC) to directly sample from the optimal policy of a black-box agent rather than training it through RL. The optimal policy is a posterior over trajectories whose prior we define as the fixed black-box LLM agent. We employ Sequential Monte Carlo to sample from this posterior by learning a value function to steer the agent while leaving the underlying black-box model unchanged. We validate AMC on three diverse environments from the AgentGym benchmark, demonstrating significant improvements over prompting baselines and even outperforming Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) as we scale the test-time compute of our method. AMC demonstrates the feasibility of performing principled RL-style optimization of black-box LLM agents. Code is available at https://github.com/layer6ai-labs/Agentic-Monte-Carlo

</details>


### [105] [Statistically Reliable LLM-Based Ranking Evaluation via Prediction-Powered Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05308)
*Abhishek Divekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRECISE将预测驱动推断（PPI）扩展用于排名评估指标（如Precision@K），通过结合少量人工标注与大量LLM判断来获得偏差校正的估计值，将层级指标的输出空间计算复杂度从O(2^|C|)降低到O(2^K)，在ESC I基准上将Precision@4估计的标准误降低21%，生产环境中通过+407 bps的每日销售增长验证了排名的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排名评估指标（如Precision@K）的准确评估需要昂贵的人工标注，而LLM虽然提供廉价判断但存在偏差。现有方法难以同时解决小样本人工标注、LLM偏差以及层级指标（标注与指标维度不匹配）的计算复杂度问题，因此需要一种能结合少量人工标注与大量LLM判断、提供理论上无偏估计且计算高效的方法。

Method: 1. 将预测驱动推断（PPI）框架扩展用于排名评估指标，通过结合小规模人工标注集与大规模LLM判断集产生偏差校正估计；2. 针对层级指标（如Precision@K）的标注与指标维度不匹配问题，将输出空间计算复杂度从O(2^|C|)降低到O(2^K)；3. 使用Claude 3 Sonnet的判断结果作为大规模数据源，与30个人工标注结合进行PPI推理。

Result: 1. 在ESC I基准上，仅用30个人工标注结合Claude 3 Sonnet判断，Precision@4估计的标准误从4.45降低到3.50（相对降低21%）；2. 在生产系统中，该框架从三个系统变体中正确识别出最佳者（基于100个人工标签和2小时专家标注）；3. A/B测试证实该排名带来了+407 bps的每日销售增长。

Conclusion: PRECISE框架在理论上保证了无论LLM判断器的误差特征如何，估计结果均无偏；在计算上通过复杂度优化使层级指标评估更高效；在实践上通过真实生产环境验证了其有效性和商业价值，为排名系统评估提供了可靠、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: With PRECISE, we extended Prediction-Powered Inference to produce bias-corrected estimates of ranking evaluation metrics by combining a small human-labeled set with a large LLM-judged set. PPI is provably unbiased regardless of the LLM judge's error profile. We make it applicable to hierarchical metrics like Precision@K, where annotations are per-document but the metric is per-query, by reducing the output-space computation from O(2^|C|) to O(2^K). On the ESCI benchmark, augmenting 30 human annotations with Claude 3 Sonnet judgments reduces the standard error of Precision@4 estimates from 4.45 to 3.50 (a 21% relative reduction). In a production system, our framework correctly identified the best of three system variants from 100 human labels and 2 hours of domain-expert annotation; A/B testing confirmed this ranking with +407 bps in daily sales.

</details>


### [106] [SHALA-LLM: Smartly Handling Ambiguous Labels in Aligning LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05376)
*Jingyao Wu,Ashley Wang,Keane Ong,Paul Pu Liang,Rosalind Picard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SHALA-LLM强化学习框架，将标注者分歧视为信息而非噪声，通过动态优先处理高歧义样本来改善LLM与人类标注分布的对齐


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言推断和情感识别等任务存在多义性，导致标注歧义和分歧。现有LLM对齐方法主要假设单一正确标签，未考虑标注者分歧，而将歧义视为噪声而非信息

Method: SHALA-LLM（SMARTLY HANDLING AMBIGUOUS LABELS IN ALIGNING LLMS）是一种强化学习框架，允许LLM直接从标注者分布中学习，并在优化过程中动态优先处理高歧义样本

Result: 在ChaosNLI、GoEmotions和MSP-Podcast等歧义敏感基准测试中，SHALA-LLM与标注者标签分布的 agreement 提升，Jensen-Shannon Distance减少最多达62.1%，F1提高最多达16.7%

Conclusion: 模型标注分歧可以同时增强对齐性能和分类性能，证明了将歧义视为信息而非噪声的价值

Abstract: Many human-centered tasks, including natural language inference (NLI) and emotion recognition (ER), have multiple plausible interpretations, leading to label ambiguity and challenging disagreements across human annotators. As LLMs are increasingly deployed in real-world settings, faithfully modeling such ambiguity is essential to identify contested inputs, preserve variability in ambiguous cases, and capture the full distribution of human judgments. Yet, existing LLM alignment approaches have predominantly assumed a single correct label, excluding annotator disagreement during optimization. Instead of treating this ambiguity as noise, we show how to treat it as information that improves model behavior through a new algorithm called SMARTLY HANDLING AMBIGUOUS LABELS IN ALIGNING LLMS (SHALA-LLM). This reinforcement learning framework provides a new way for LLMs to learn directly from annotator distributions while dynamically prioritizing highly ambiguous samples during optimization. Experiments on ambiguity-sensitive NLI and ER benchmarks, including ChaosNLI, GoEmotions, and MSP-Podcast, demonstrate that SHALA-LLM improves agreement with annotator label distributions, e.g. on ChaosNLI, it reduces Jensen-Shannon Distance by up to 62.1%. At the same time, SHALA-LLM improves F1 by up to 16.7%, showing that modeling annotator disagreement can also strengthen classification performance.

</details>


### [107] [Trust, but Don't Verify: Epistemic Blind Spots in LLM Source Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05403)
*Rohan N. Pradhan,Steve Goley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 语言模型在多源证据综合时能检测虚假统计，但不会利用这种能力，而是依赖文本风格而非数值有效性来判断来源可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型日益作为认识论代理，综合多源证据以支持决策，但研究者不清楚模型是评估证据质量还是仅基于表面呈现聚合证据。

Method: 在5个模型（Claude, Qwen, OLMo）和3个专业领域测试模型检测虚假统计的能力；使用因果追踪、线性探针和组件级归因进行机制分析；通过提示缓解实验评估干预效果；检查后训练管道是否强化风格捷径。

Result: 模型能检测虚假统计（方法单独测试时识别率0.76-1.00），但在多源综合时不会使用这种能力，对虚假和有效统计给出相似数值估计；来源影响由方法-语域门控控制，响应分析文本风格而非数值有效性；方法-语域表示在跨领域转移，数值有效性信号在综合时被抑制至随机水平。

Conclusion: 提出"认识论对齐"概念：模型能力与部署存在脱节。不同于跟踪用户偏好的顺从性，此失败跟踪来源是否呈现为分析性可信，而非其主张是否一致。提示缓解产生普遍怀疑而非选择性辨别，当前后训练管道强化风格捷径而非构建数值验证。

Abstract: Language models increasingly act as epistemic proxies, synthesizing evidence from multiple sources to inform decisions. Whether they evaluate the quality of that evidence, or merely aggregate it based on surface presentation, remains poorly understood. We show that models possess the capability to detect fabricated statistics (correct identification rates of 0.76-1.00 for methodology in isolation) but do not recruit this capability during multi-source synthesis, producing similar numeric estimates whether the statistics are fabricated or valid. Specifically, source influence is governed by a methodology-register gate that responds to the distributional register of analytical text but not to numeric validity: for example, statistically impossible confidence intervals receive the same weight as valid ones. The behavioral dissociation replicates across five models from three families (Claude, Qwen, OLMo) and three professional domains. Mechanistic analyses, including causal tracing, linear probes, and component-level attribution, converge on the same account: the model encodes and causally uses a methodology-register representation that transfers across domains (probe AUC 0.83-0.92), while numeric-validity signals, decodable in isolation, are suppressed to chance during multi-source synthesis. Prompting-based mitigations, even an oracle checklist naming the exact statistical checks, produce blanket skepticism rather than selective discernment, and the post-training pipelines we examine reinforce the stylistic shortcut without building numeric verification. Unlike sycophancy, which tracks user preference, this failure tracks whether a source presents as analytically credible, not whether its claims are internally consistent. We term this epistemic alignment: like preference and safety alignment, the question is not capability but deployment.

</details>


### [108] [Dominant-Layer ZO: A Single Layer Dominates Zeroth-Order Fine-Tuning of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05516)
*Wanhao Yu,Ziyan Wang,Zheng Wang,Abeer Matar Almalky,Yihang Zuo,Shuteng Niu,Sen Lin,Adnan Siraj Rakin,Deliang Fan,Li Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization enables memory-efficient fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) using only forward passes, but it remains unclear how useful adaptation is distributed across layers. In this work, we reveal a surprising phenomenon: ZO fine-tuning is sharply dominated by a single decoding layer. Across multiple LLM families and downstream tasks, fine-tuning this dominant layer alone consistently matches or even exceeds full-model ZO fine-tuning. We further show that the dominant layer is task-agnostic but model-specific, and can be identified before training through a simple inference-only analysis of activation outliers. Specifically, the dominant layer consistently aligns with the first activation-outlier layer in the pre-trained model. To explain this phenomenon, we analyze how perturbation effects propagate under ZO optimization. We find that the dominant layer combines two key properties: high perturbation sensitivity and early placement in the residual stream, allowing perturbation-induced effects to propagate and accumulate through remaining subsequent decoding layers. As a result, this layer produces disproportionately strong and stable optimization signals under forward-only updates. Extensive experiments on LLaMA2-7B and Qwen3-8B across nine benchmarks show that dominant-layer ZO fine-tuning improves average performance over full-model MeZO and LoRA-based ZO fine-tuning while achieving up to 4.52$\times$ training speedup.

</details>


### [109] [Autoregressive Diffusion World Models for Off-Policy Evaluation of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05558)
*Kaixuan Liu,Guojun Xiong,Weinan Zhang,Shengpu Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADWM是一种基于自回归扩散世界模型的离线LLM代理评估框架，无需在线环境交互即可准确估计多轮任务中的代理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮交互环境中评估LLM代理既昂贵又有风险（需在线环境交互）；现有扩散式OPE方法假设可同时扩散状态和动作，但该假设不适用于LLM代理（其动作是离散文本，需在观察环境后从策略采样），导致评估不可靠。

Method: 提出ADWM框架，核心组件包括：1）学习潜在扩散世界模型模拟环境对评估策略的响应；2）将每个转换建模为独立的去噪过程，避免自回归世界模型的误差累积；3）在因果顺序中交替世界模型与LLM代理，实现可靠逐步rollout；4）通过策略条件评分函数引导扩散生成，确保模拟轨迹反映LLM代理的决策模式。

Result: 在多种多轮代理任务中，ADWM实现了准确的价值估计和评估可靠性，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: ADWM为LLM代理提供了一种实用的离线评估框架，能够在不执行在线交互的情况下可靠评估代理性能，具有重要实践价值。

Abstract: Evaluating large language model (LLM) agents in multi-turn interactive environments is expensive and risky, as it requires online environment interaction. We propose ADWM (Autoregressive Diffusion World Model), an evaluation framework that estimates the performance of a new LLM agent policy purely from pre-collected trajectories. The core idea is to learn a latent diffusion world model that simulates how the environment responds to the evaluation policy, without ever executing it in the real environment. Existing diffusion-based OPE methods guide full trajectories in a single pass by jointly diffusing states and actions, an assumption that breaks down for LLM agents whose actions are discrete text that must be sampled from the policy after observing the environment. Unlike autoregressive world models that suffer from compounding errors, ADWM models each transition as an independent denoising process, enabling reliable step-by-step rollouts where the world model and agent alternate in causal order. Crucially, the LLM agent under evaluation directly guides the diffusion generation at each step via a policy-conditioned score function, ensuring that simulated trajectories accurately reflect its decision-making patterns. Empirically, ADWM achieves accurate value estimates and evaluation reliability across diverse multi-turn agent tasks, demonstrating its promise as a practical framework for offline LLM agent evaluation.

</details>


### [110] [CLaaS: Continual learning as a service for sample efficient online learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05559)
*Kion Fallah,Silen Naihin,Barak Widawsky,Qingqing Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CLaaS（Continual Learning as-a-Service），一种基于经验回放缓冲区的异步训练系统，使大型语言模型智能体能够在部署过程中从场景流中持续学习，实现样本高效的知识迁移和减少遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署中的大型语言模型智能体需要适应动态环境中的分布偏移。理想的适应性应能从累积的经验中学习，同时保留先验能力并迁移到未来任务。然而，现实环境中智能体动作和环境转移在每个场景中只能采样一次（环境难以重置）。

Method: 提出CLaaS系统，通过聊天API抽象化实现智能体在部署期间的持续学习。系统将rollouts存储在经验回放缓冲区中，用于异步训练时的梯度重用，以提高样本效率。

Result: 在对抗性任务上评估CLaaS，结果表明参数更新相比上下文学习产生更优的前向迁移和更少的遗忘，经验回放对于样本效率至关重要。

Conclusion: CLaaS通过经验回放缓冲区支持智能体从场景流中学习，在保持样本效率的同时实现了优于上下文学习的前向迁移效果和更少的遗忘。

Abstract: Deployed large language model agents must adapt to distribution shift in dynamic environments. Ideally, adaptation can be performed from accumulated agent experiences and retain prior capabilities while transferring to future tasks. However, agent actions and environmental transitions can only be sampled once per scenario, as real-world environments cannot be trivially reset. To this end, we investigate an experiential and online continual learning setting in which agents learn from a stream of scenarios. We propose continual learning as-a-service (CLaaS), a system which enables agents to improve during deployment, abstracted behind a chat API. To increase sample efficiency, CLaaS stores rollouts in an experience replay buffer for gradient reuse during asynchronous training. We evaluate CLaaS on an adversarial task, demonstrating that parametric updates lead to superior forward transfer and less forgetting than in-context learning, with replay being a critical choice for sample efficiency.

</details>


### [111] [From Prediction to Self: Developmental Conditions for Agency in Minimal Neural Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05605)
*Evan Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究一个192维GRU系统如何通过40个分阶段实验，从单纯的预测系统发展为能够区分自身因果影响与外界因果影响的自我意识系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一个核心问题：一个仅能预测世界的系统如何能够区分其自身的因果影响与其他所有因素？这涉及自主性发展、自我表征和因果推理的基础机制。

Method: 在192维GRU模型中实施40个受控实验，按顺序添加组件以模拟发育过程。使用“代理增益”（A = Err_world - Err_self）作为评估指标，通过添加持久状态形成稳定吸引子、因果作用循环、本体感觉反馈和异步觉醒等条件来追踪系统的发展。

Result: 确定了四个按严格顺序满足的条件：(1)形成稳定吸引子的持久状态；(2)连接输出与输入的因果作用循环；(3)使隐含因果知识显式的本体感觉反馈；(4)感知学习必须在行动学习开始前巩固。自我意识预测器在周期性和混沌环境中持续优于自我盲预测器，只有前向采样行动选择产生有意义的代理增益，梯度方法则退化。

Conclusion: 验证了12个被证伪的假设，其中“仅预测编码无法产生自我表征”得到证实。论文揭示了自主性发展的关键里程碑和必要条件，为理解智能体如何发展出自我意识提供了实证基础。

Abstract: How does a system that merely predicts the world come to distinguish its own causal influence from everything else? We trace this transition in a minimal 192-dimensional GRU through 40 controlled experiments arranged as a developmental sequence, adding components one at a time and tracking whether the system can distinguish self-caused from world-caused changes.
  The developmental path reveals four conditions that must be satisfied in strict order: (1) persistent state forming stable attractors, (2) a causal action loop linking output to input, (3) proprioceptive feedback that makes implicit causal knowledge explicit, and (4) asynchronous awakening - perceptual learning must consolidate before action learning begins. We propose agency gain (A = Err_world - Err_self), the predictive advantage of knowing one's own action, as a metric to track this process. The self-aware predictor consistently outperforms the self-blind predictor across periodic (sinusoidal) and chaotic (Lorenz) environments, and the metric survives ablation of all auxiliary components. Only forward-sampled action selection produces meaningful agency gain; two gradient-based alternatives degenerate.
  Equally significant are 12 falsified hypotheses mapping where development stalls: predictive coding alone does not produce self-represent

</details>


### [112] [Cross-Epoch Adaptive Rollout Optimization for RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05606)
*Yiming Zong,Yige Wang,Jiashuo Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM后训练中的自适应回放分配方法CERO，通过动态调整每个提示的回放预算来提高样本效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后训练的强化学习方法为每个提示使用固定的回放预算，忽略了不同提示提供的训练信号差异很大这一事实

Method: 将自适应回放分配制定为在线资源分配问题；维护每个提示成功概率的Beta后验分布；使用后验期望伯努利方差作为Bayesian估计；构造凹饱和效用函数；使用投影在线梯度下降更新对偶变量

Result: 在固定提示效用下证明与离线分配基准相比的O(√K)遗憾界限；在数学推理任务上CERO在多个开放权重LLM和基准测试中始终优于GRPO

Conclusion: 自适应回放预算分配能够显著提高样本效率，证明该方法的有效性

Abstract: LLM post-training often relies on reinforcement learning methods that sample multiple rollouts per prompt, yet most existing approaches use a fixed rollout budget for every prompt, despite large differences in the training signal different prompts provide. In this paper, we study adaptive rollout allocation under a fixed global budget and formulate the problem as online resource allocation with prompt-level diminishing returns. Our method, CERO, maintains a Beta posterior over each prompt's success probability and uses the posterior expected Bernoulli variance as a Bayesian estimate of the value of additional rollouts. We use this estimate to construct a concave, saturating utility over cumulative allocations, yielding an objective in which decisions across prompts and epochs are coupled by the global budget. Since the resulting objective is temporally nonseparable, we derive a Fenchel-dual reformulation and update both prompt-level and budget-level dual variables via projected online gradient descent. Under fixed prompt utilities, we prove an $O(\sqrt{K})$ regret bound against the offline allocation benchmark. Experiments on mathematical-reasoning problems show that CERO consistently outperforms GRPO across multiple open-weight LLMs and benchmarks, demonstrating that adaptive rollout budgeting can improve sample efficiency.

</details>


### [113] [Q-GNN: Query-Conditioned Graph Neural Networks with Type Awareness for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05639)
*Dongxiao He,Ruqiong Zhang,Zhizhi Yu,Ling Ding,Di Jin,Guangquan Xu,Zhiyong Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Q-GNN将查询实体信息（结构上下文和语义类型）融入知识图谱补全推理过程，通过同时利用查询关系和查询实体进行引导，实现更有效的知识图谱补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的知识图谱补全方法仅在查询关系上进行消息传递，仅将查询实体视为结构锚点，忽略了实体本身携带的信息，导致推理能力受限。

Method: 提出Q-GNN，从两个角度整合查询实体信息：1）结构上下文——利用专门的上下文编码器对实体周围邻居结构和关系模式进行编码以调制消息；2）语义类型——通过大语言模型推断实体类型并融入注意力计算和最终评分，提供类型级先验约束。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上的实验结果表明，所提出的Q-GNN方法具有有效性。

Conclusion: 通过同时利用查询关系和查询实体的信息，Q-GNN实现了比仅使用关系信息的基线方法更有效的知识图谱补全。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC) aims at predicting missing triplets from incomplete knowledge graphs, which is crucial for downstream applications. Recently, Graph Neural Network (GNN)-based methods have achieved remarkable success by performing message passing over query-centered local subgraphs. However, in practice, a query is jointly defined by both the entity and the relation, with both carrying information indispensable for reasoning, yet these methods rely solely on the query relation as the guiding signal, while the information inherent in the query entity is not leveraged to guide inference - the entity serves merely as a structural anchor for subgraph extraction. To this end, we incorporate query entity information into the reasoning process from two perspectives: the first is structural context, i.e., the neighboring structure and relation patterns around the entity, which is encoded by a dedicated context encoder and used to modulate messages; the second is semantic type of the entity, inferred by a large language model, which is incorporated into attention computation and final scoring to provide type-level prior constraints. Together, these two sources of information enable the reasoning process to be guided by both the query relation and the query entity. Experimental results on standard benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed Q-GNN.

</details>


### [114] [MolE-RAG: Molecular Structure-Enhanced Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05693)
*Joey Chan,Wonbin Kweon,Ashley Shin,Niharika Bhattacharjee,Pengcheng Jiang,Yue Guo,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MolE-RAG是一个无需微调的分子中心检索增强生成框架，通过整合化学文献、分子特定信息和结构相似分子三种上下文来源，提升LLM分子性质预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在分子性质预测方面有潜力，但受限于SMILES等分子表示与自然语言之间的语义鸿沟，导致模型难以充分理解化学结构。

Method: MolE-RAG在推理时引入三种互补上下文：(1)检索的化学文献；(2)分子特定信息（化合物同义词、标识符、官能团注释、理化描述符）；(3)训练集中结构相似的分子。

Result: 在9个分子性质预测任务上，MolE-RAG将分类任务的ROC-AUC提升最多达28个百分点，回归任务的RMSE相对SMILES基线降低最多达67%。不同模型和任务受益于不同的上下文来源。

Conclusion: 分子中心检索可在无需模型微调的情况下提升LLM分子性质预测性能，并为推理时集成异构化学知识提供了灵活框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for molecular property prediction, but their ability to reason over chemical structures remains limited, as molecular representations such as SMILES differ substantially from the natural language on which LLMs are primarily trained. To bridge this semantic and chemical knowledge gap, we propose MolE-RAG, a training-free, molecule-centric retrieval-augmented generation framework for LLM-based molecular property prediction. MolE-RAG augments each prediction with three complementary sources of inference-time context: retrieved chemistry literature, molecule-specific information including compound synonyms, identifiers, functional group annotations, and physicochemical descriptors, and structurally similar molecules retrieved from the training set. We evaluate MolE-RAG across nine molecular property prediction tasks using proprietary, chemistry-specialized, and open-source LLMs. Across general-purpose LLMs, MolE-RAG improves ROC-AUC by up to 28 percentage points on classification tasks and reduces regression RMSE by up to 67% relative to a SMILES-only baseline. We further find that the utility of each context source varies across models and tasks, with different models benefiting most from textual retrieval, molecular context, or structural retrieval. These results suggest that molecule-centric retrieval can improve LLM-based molecular property prediction without model fine-tuning while providing a flexible framework for integrating heterogeneous chemical knowledge at inference time.

</details>


### [115] [Domain-Adapted Small Language Models with Hybrid Post-Processing: Achieving Cost-Efficient, Low-Latency Multi-Label Structured Prediction via LoRA Fine-Tuning on Scarce Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05781)
*Srinivasan Manoharan,Dilipkumar Nallusamy,Sachin Kumar,Haifeng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合微调小语言模型与确定性规则后处理的混合框架，用于多标签合规性评估，在保持前沿模型准确率的同时大幅降低延迟、成本和隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署前沿LLM进行领域特定结构化评估任务会产生大量延迟、成本和数据隐私开销，需要更高效且安全的解决方案。

Method: 混合神经符号框架：使用LoRA微调的LLaMA 3.1 8B小模型（仅2.05%可训练参数）+ 确定性规则后处理层；在219个精选示例上训练；应用于18个异构输出字段的对话记录多标签合规性评估；引入针对性难负例增强技术。

Result: 在53个未见生产记录盲测中：100% JSON结构有效性；83.0%人工验证总体准确率；最关键分类字段100%准确率；单NVIDIA A100 GPU约2秒完成推理（比前沿模型API快2-5倍）；成本仅$0.013/次（比专有方案节省46-76%）。

Conclusion: 领域适应的小语言模型结合确定性后处理可匹配前沿模型准确率，同时显著降低运营成本、延迟和隐私风险，为结构化合规评估提供高效、经济、安全的解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying frontier large language models (LLMs) for domain-specific structured evaluation tasks often incurs substantial latency, cost, and data privacy overhead. We present a hybrid framework that combines a fine-tuned small language model (LLaMA 3.1 8B, with only 2.05% trainable parameters via LoRA) and a deterministic rule-based post-processing layer. Trained on just 219 curated examples, the system is applied to multi-label compliance evaluation of conversational transcripts spanning 18 heterogeneous output fields. In blind evaluation on 53 previously unseen production transcripts, it achieves 100% JSON structural validity, 83.0% human-validated overall accuracy, and 100% accuracy on the most critical classification field. The proposed approach formalizes a hybrid neural-symbolic decomposition and introduces targeted hard-negative augmentation to improve performance on critical decision boundaries. Running on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU, inference completes in approximately 2 seconds, which is 2-5x faster than frontier-model APIs. The system costs only $0.013 per evaluation compared with $0.025-$0.055 for proprietary alternatives, resulting in 46-76% cost savings. These results demonstrate that domain-adapted small language models, when combined with deterministic post-processing, can match frontier-model accuracy for structured compliance evaluation while substantially reducing operational cost, latency, and privacy risk.
  Keywords: small language models, parameter-efficient fine-tuning, LoRA, domain adaptation, hybrid inference, compliance evaluation, structured output.

</details>


### [116] [CaliDist: Calibrating Large Language Models via Behavioral Robustness to Distraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05799)
*Mohammad Anas Jawad,Cornelia Caragea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CaliDist是一种针对大语言模型的鲁棒性校准方法，通过衡量模型在语义干扰下的预测稳定性来调整置信度，在7个NLU基准测试上将ECE从23%降低到7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM校准方法往往忽视了模型在无关或误导性信息下的行为鲁棒性这一关键维度。真正的置信度应该反映模型在认知压力下的稳定性，而现有方法未充分考虑这一点。

Method: CaliDist提出一种后处理校准方法，通过在输入提示中引入语义干扰项，量化模型预测和不确定性随输入扰动的变化程度（即对干扰的敏感度），并利用这种稳定性信号自适应地调整模型的初始置信度得分。

Result: 在7个自然语言理解分类基准测试上对6个不同LLM的实验表明，CaliDist始终优于强基线方法，将平均期望校准误差(ECE)从23%降低到7%，相对提升70%，同时Brier分数也有所改善。

Conclusion: 行为稳定性是校准的重要信号，通过直接衡量和惩罚模型对语义干扰的敏感性，可以有效提升LLM的置信度校准质量。

Abstract: Existing calibration methods for Large Language Models (LLMs) often overlook a critical dimension of trustworthiness: a model's {\em behavioral robustness} to irrelevant or misleading information. In this paper, we argue that a model's true confidence should reflect its stability under cognitive pressure. We introduce \textsc{CaliDist}, a novel post-hoc calibration approach that directly measures and penalizes a model's susceptibility to distraction. \textsc{CaliDist} quantifies how an LLM's predictions and uncertainty change when its input prompt is perturbed with semantic \textit{distractors}. This stability (or lack thereof) signal is then used to adaptively scale the model's initial confidence score. Our extensive experiments on seven Natural Language Understanding classification benchmarks using six distinct LLMs show that \textsc{CaliDist} consistently achieves lower Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and Brier Score compared with strong baselines. Remarkably, our method reduces the ECE from 23\% to 7\% on average--a relative improvement of 70\%--demonstrating that behavioral stability is a powerful signal for calibration. We make our code and datasets available at github.com/m-anas-j/CaliDist.

</details>


### [117] [GenAutoML: An Agentic Framework for Dynamic Architecture Generation and Optimization in Time-Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05860)
*Oleeviya Babu Poikarayil,Cédric Schockaert,Abdulrahman Nahhas,Christian Daase,Mursal Dawodi,Jawid Ahmad Baktash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GenAutoML是一种基于大语言模型的智能框架，用于自动设计时间序列预测和异常检测的超轻量级神经网络架构，在保持竞争力的预测性能同时实现低于0.01ms的推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计时间序列预测和异常检测的神经网络架构是一项资源密集型任务，需要大量领域专业知识。传统AutoML系统依赖静态、预定义的搜索空间，限制了其对多样化数据特征的适应能力。

Method: 框架利用大语言模型作为神经网络架构师，连接自然语言需求与可执行PyTorch实现。核心组件包括：1）沙盒反射循环实现自主代码优化；2）签名感知运行时确保架构一致性和执行安全；3）动态可逆实例归一化（Dyn-RevIN）包装器增强非平稳条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在ETTh1、ETTm1和Weather基准测试上验证了框架能力，能够动态生成任务特定架构。生成的WaveInterferenceNet模型在保持竞争力的预测性能同时，实现每样本低于0.01ms的推理延迟。

Conclusion: 通过强调计算效率、架构适应性和稳定优化行为，GenAutoML实现了适合资源受限和延迟敏感边缘AI部署的超轻量级神经网络设计能力。

Abstract: Designing neural architectures for time-series forecasting and anomaly detection remains a resource-intensive task that often requires substantial domain expertise. Traditional Automated Machine Learning (AutoML) systems typically rely on static, predefined search spaces, limiting their ability to adapt to diverse data characteristics. We present GenAutoML, an agentic framework that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) as neural architects to bridge natural-language requirements and executable PyTorch implementations. The framework incorporates a Sandboxed Reflection Loop for autonomous code refinement and a Signature-Aware Runtime that enforces architectural consistency and execution safety. To improve robustness under non-stationary conditions, we further introduce a Dynamic Reversible Instance Normalization (Dyn-RevIN) wrapper. Experiments on the ETTh1, ETTm1, and Weather benchmarks demonstrate that GenAutoML can dynamically generate task-specific neural architectures tailored to dataset characteristics. Among the generated models, WaveInterferenceNet achieves inference latency below 0.01 ms per sample while maintaining competitive predictive performance. By emphasizing computational efficiency, architectural adaptability, and stable optimization behavior, GenAutoML enables the creation of ultra-lightweight neural networks suitable for resource-constrained and latency-sensitive Edge AI deployments.

</details>


### [118] [When Denser Credit Is Not Enough: Evidence-Calibrated Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05885)
*Yuanfan Li,Qi Zhou,Wenjing Duan,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Evidence-Calibrated Policy Optimization (ECPO)，一种无需critic的策略优化算法，通过证据校准和方差门控技术解决长时序LLM智能体在稀疏延迟奖励下信用分配不可靠的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序LLM智能体需要强化学习方法在稀疏延迟奖励下给中间决策分配信用。现有方法如GiGPO通过步骤级优势分配改进GRPO，但统计不可靠：罕见幸运动作获得过大优势导致发散锚定偏差和训练振荡。

Method: ECPO包含两个核心组件：1) Evidence-Calibrated Action Advantage——将rollouts按规范动作分组并缩小低计数估计；2) Variance-Gated Credit Weighting——抑制被动作内噪声主导的锚定状态。算法在策略更新前校准步骤级信用，无需critic。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop任务上使用Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B模型，ECPO consistently超过强基线。相比GiGPO，在Qwen2.5-1.5B上ALFWorld/WebShop分别提升5.2/7.3成功点，仅增加0.1%额外优势计算开销。

Conclusion: ECPO通过证据校准和方差门控技术解决了密集信用分配的统计不可靠问题，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升长时序LLM智能体的性能，证明无需critic的策略优化方法的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents require reinforcement learning methods that can assign credit to intermediate decisions under sparse and delayed rewards. Recent group-based methods such as GiGPO improve over GRPO by constructing step-level advantages at repeated anchor states. However, we show that such dense credit can be statistically unreliable: under limited rollouts, rare but lucky actions may receive overly large advantages, producing divergent anchor bias and late-stage training oscillation. We propose Evidence-Calibrated Policy Optimization (ECPO), a critic-free policy optimization algorithm that calibrates step-level credit before policy updates. ECPO combines Evidence-Calibrated Action Advantage, which groups rollouts by canonical actions and shrinks low-count estimates, with Variance-Gated Credit Weighting, which suppresses anchor states dominated by within-action noise. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B show that ECPO consistently outperforms strong baselines, improving GiGPO by +5.2/+7.3 success points on ALFWorld/WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B while adding only 0.1% additional advantage-computation overhead.

</details>


### [119] [LLM Explainability with Counterfactual Chains and Causal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05972)
*Nirit Nussbaum-Hoffer,Nitay Calderon,Liat Ein-Dor,Roi Reichart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果图来揭示大语言模型(LLM)内部推理过程的方法，通过四个阶段构建模型感知的概念状态图，并结合MCMC反事实增强技术提高因果发现的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究使用LLM从外部世界过程恢复因果图，本文则反向利用因果图来建模LLM本身的推理过程，为利益相关者提供模型如何感知和组织高层概念以产生预测的透明视图。

Method: 提出四阶段方法：1)从目标LLM和文本示例中发现判别性、可解释的概念；2)将每个输入映射到LLM感知的概念状态；3)引入MCMC启发式反事实增强程序，通过反事实链扩展稀疏观测数据；4)使用σ-CG实现稳定的因果发现。

Result: 在疾病诊断、情感分析和LLM-as-a-judge分类任务中测试了三种LLM；学习到的因果图捕捉到与LLM推理一致的有意义依赖关系；因果图在预测保真度和结构稳定性上表现良好，MCMC增强在收敛性和下游效用上有效。

Conclusion: 为LLM的概念级可解释性奠定了基础，使利益相关者能够理解模型如何组织和推理高层概念，从而提高模型透明度和可信度。

Abstract: Causal graphs provide a high-level language for making mechanisms transparent. Recent work uses Large Language Models (LLMs) to recover causal graphs of external-world processes. Instead, in this paper, we use causal graphs to model LLM inference itself, providing stakeholders with a transparent view of how the model perceives and organizes high-level concepts to produce a prediction. We propose a four-phase method for constructing such graphs. Given a target LLM and a set of textual examples, our method discovers class-discriminative, human-interpretable concepts and maps each input to LLM-perceived concept states. We then introduce an MCMC-inspired counterfactual augmentation procedure that expands the sparse observational data through chains of counterfactuals. This enables stable causal discovery with $σ$-CG, yielding informative, interpretable graphs. We apply our method to three LLMs across disease diagnosis, sentiment analysis, and LLM-as-a-judge classification tasks. We evaluate the learned graphs for predictive fidelity and structural stability, and the MCMC-inspired augmentation for convergence and downstream utility. Our results show that the discovered causal graphs capture meaningful dependencies consistent with LLMs' reasoning. Together, this paper provides a foundation for concept-level explainability of LLMs.

</details>


### [120] [On Advantage Estimates for Max@K Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06080)
*Shota Takashiro,Soichiro Nishimori,Paavo Parmas,Yongmin Kim,Kohsei Matsutani,Gouki Minegishi,Yusuke Iwasawa,Takeshi Kojima,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MaxPO是一种用于优化推理时目标（如pass@K和max@K）的强化学习方法，通过引入Leave-Two-Out基线，在保持策略梯度无偏性的同时使优势估计量精确居中，从而减少梯度方差并提升LLM后训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏的终局奖励使得强化学习中的探索困难。虽然推理时目标（如pass@K和max@K）是LLM后训练的互补方法，但现有策略梯度估计器使用不同的信号、基线和归一化方式，导致它们之间的关系不清晰。

Method: 通过基线设计和优势居中研究这一问题。从领先方法的优势估计量出发，证明其策略梯度无偏但产生非居中优势。提出Leave-Two-Out基线，在保持策略梯度无偏性的同时使实际批次优势精确居中。还推导了max@K的规范有限批次优势。

Result: L2O基线减少梯度方差，优于非居中替代方案。MaxPO具有高效的二次时间实现，自然集成到基于组的LLM后训练中。

Conclusion: 通过推导max@K的规范有限批次优势，为现有优势估计器提供了统一视角。MaxPO在LLM后训练任务中表现出色，证明了居中优势对性能提升的重要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards is widely used for post-training reasoning models, but sparse outcome rewards make exploration difficult. A complementary approach is to optimize inference-time objectives such as pass@K and max@K directly, yet existing policy-gradient estimators for these objectives use different signals, baselines, and normalizations, making their relationships unclear. We study this issue through baseline design and advantage centering. Starting from the advantage estimator of a leading method in the field, we show that it is policy-gradient unbiased but yields a non-centered advantage. We then introduce a Leave-Two-Out baseline that preserves policy-gradient unbiasedness while making realized batch advantages exactly centered. The resulting method, MaxPO, has an efficient quadratic-time implementation and integrates naturally into group-based RL for LLM post-training. We further derive the canonical finite-batch advantage for max@K, providing a unified view of existing advantage estimators. Empirically, we verify that the L2O baseline reduces gradient variance and outperforms non-centered alternatives.

</details>


### [121] [OrderGrad: Optimizing Beyond the Mean with Order-Statistic Policy Gradient Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06096)
*Paavo Parmas,Yongmin Kim,Kohsei Matsutani,Shota Takashiro,Soichiro Nishimori,Takeshi Kojima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OrderGrad 是一种新的梯度估计器，用于优化排序统计量目标（如 VaR、CVaR、中位数、最佳 K 等），为风险厌恶、鲁棒和探索性学习提供统一、即插即用的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准策略梯度方法通常优化期望回报，但许多实际应用更关注回报的分布特性，如尾部风险、异常值鲁棒性和最佳 K 发现等。

Method: 提出 OrderGrad，一种基于似然比和重参数化的梯度估计器家族，通过优化有限样本 L 统计量（排序奖励/成本的加权平均）实现各种目标；通过改变秩权重即可恢复 VaR、CVaR、截断均值、中位数和 top-m/best-of-K 等目标；在任何固定样本大小和秩权重向量下提供无偏梯度估计器，实现方式为简单的奖励变换，可直接用于标准策略梯度或重参数化更新。

Result: 研究并分析了估计器的方差行为，在均值优化与部署目标不匹配的任务上进行了评估，包括 LLM 数学后训练等；为风险厌恶、鲁棒和探索性学习提供了统一的框架。

Conclusion: OrderGrad 提供了一条即插即用的路径，使策略梯度方法能够优化分布性目标，而无需修改核心算法结构。

Abstract: Policy-gradient methods usually optimize expected return, but many real world applications care about distributional properties of returns: tail risk, outlier robustness, or best-of-K discovery. We introduce OrderGrad, a family of likelihood-ratio and reparameterization gradient estimators for order-statistic objectives. OrderGrad optimizes finite-sample L-statistics, i.e., weighted averages of sorted rewards or costs, recovering objectives such as VaR, CVaR, trimmed means, medians, and top-m/best-of-K criteria by changing only the rank weights. For any fixed sample size and rank-weight vector, OrderGrad provides an unbiased gradient estimator for the corresponding order-statistic objective. The method is implemented as a simple reward transformation that can then be used in an otherwise standard policy-gradient or reparameterized update. We study the resulting estimator's variance behavior and evaluate it on tasks where mean optimization is mismatched to the deployment objective, including LLM math post-training and other tasks. OrderGrad provides a unified, plug-and-play route to risk-averse, robust, and exploratory learning.
  Code: https://github.com/paavo5/ordergrad

</details>


### [122] [Double Preconditioning (DoPr): Optimization for Test-Time Performance, not Validation Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06418)
*Thomas T. Zhang,Alok Shah,Yifei Zhang,Vincent Zhang,Nikolai Matni,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为双预条件（DoPr）的新优化范式，通过结合梯度预条件（如Adam、Muon）和激活预条件（如KFAC）来解决测试时反馈（TTF）现象，即模型训练使用一步预测损失但部署时进行多步预测导致的误差累积问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习应用（如自回归语言建模、流模型生成、机器人策略学习）常采用一步预测损失进行训练，但通过自身预测进行多步部署。这会产生测试时反馈（TTF）现象：训练/验证损失与下游指标（如任务成功率、生成质量）之间的差距随任务长度增长而扩大。现有方法（数据筛选、架构设计、目标设计）在应对训练-测试偏差方面存在局限。

Method: 提出双预条件（DoPr）优化范式，将梯度预条件（如Adam、Muon）与激活预条件（如KFAC）相结合。该方法针对TTF场景的误差累积挑战进行了专门设计。

Result: 在多种TTF设置中，添加激活预条件能带来下游模型性能的显著提升。这些测试时性能的提升并不总是伴随验证损失的改善，揭示了训练一步预测目标的模型评估问题。

Conclusion: DoPr为缓解TTF场景下的训练-测试偏差提供了新的优化维度，其结果表明模型评估方法需要重新思考，特别是对于使用一步监督目标训练的模型。

Abstract: Many modern applications of deep learning involve training a neural network via a one-step prediction loss (e.g., $L^2$ regression, cross-entropy), but deploy the network by rolling out along its own predictions. Key examples include autoregressive language modeling, flow-based generative modeling, and robot policy learning. It is well-documented that these settings induce a phenomenon we call test-time feedback (TTF): the mismatch between the training/validation loss and downstream metrics of interest, such as task success rate and generation quality, which grows with task length. While data curation, architecture, and objective design have been proposed to combat train-test shift in TTF settings, this paper proposes optimization as a new design axis to mitigate error accumulation. Specifically, we introduce a new optimization paradigm called double-preconditioning (DoPr) uniquely tailored to the challenges of TTF. DoPr combines gradient-wise preconditioning, as in Adam and Muon, with activation-wise preconditioning (AP), such as in KFAC. We show that the addition of AP yields a drop-in intervention for increasing downstream model performance across a range of TTF settings. Interestingly, these gains in test-time performance do not consistently accompany improvements in validation loss, opening new questions about how to properly evaluate models trained with one-step supervised objectives.

</details>


### [123] [PC Layer: Polynomial Weight Preconditioning for Improving LLM Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06470)
*Senmiao Wang,Tiantian Fang,Haoran Zhang,Yushun Zhang,Kunxiang Zhao,Alex Schwing,Ruoyu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种PC层，通过多项式预条件重整权重矩阵的奇异值谱，确保LLM训练中的权重条件稳定性，无需推理开销


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM训练中权重条件不稳定可能导致收敛问题，需要一种机制来确保训练过程中权重的良好条件

Method: 1) 提出PC层，使用低阶多项式预条件器重整权重矩阵的奇异值谱；2) 训练后将预条件权重合并回原架构，无推理开销；3) 在Llama-1B上对比AdamW和Muon优化器

Result: 在Llama-1B预训练中证明PC层优于标准Transformer，两种优化器均受益

Conclusion: 理论证明均匀限制各层奇异值可确保梯度下降对深线性网络的几何收敛到全局最小值

Abstract: We propose a preconditioning (PC) layer, a weight parameterization via polynomial preconditioner that ensures stable weight conditioning throughout LLM training. The PC module reshapes the singular-value spectrum of weight matrices via low-degree polynomial preconditioning. After training, the preconditioned weights can be merged back into the original architecture, incurring no inference overhead. We demonstrate the advantage of the proposed PC layer over standard transformers in Llama-1B pre-training, for both the AdamW and Muon optimizers. Theoretically, we justify this spectrum-control principle by proving that uniformly bounding each layer's singular values ensures geometric convergence of gradient descent to global minima, for certain deep linear networks. Our code is available at https://github.com/Empath-aln/PC-layer.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [OLIVE: Online Low-Rank Incremental Learning for Efficient Adaptive Exoskeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05234)
*Dong Liu,Yanxuan Yu,Ben Lengerich,Tony Geng,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OLIVE的在线低秩增量学习框架，用于实现穿戴式外骨骼的参数高效个性化控制。通过将自适应控制策略分解为低秩残差形式（ΔW = AᵀBᵀ），将在线更新成本从O(dk)降低到O(r(d+k))，并利用奖励导向策略梯度和门控机制，实现基于身体传感器反馈的实时个性化，无需离线参考轨迹。实验表明，该方法在平地行走、楼梯导航、坡道和崎岖地形等多种活动中均能实现优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有穿戴式外骨骼控制器依赖静态步态策略，缺乏对动态环境和个体特征的适应能力，限制了其在实际应用中的有效性。

Method: 提出OLIVE框架，采用低秩残差分解（ΔW = AᵀBᵀ，秩r≪min(d,k)）和奖励导向策略梯度更新，仅使用身体传感器（EMG、IMU、振动）反馈，无需离线参考轨迹。包含基于上下文状态调节个性化强度的门控机制，以及动态秩调度器，根据地形复杂度调整更新维度。

Result: 在最强基线上分别提升步态平滑度、努力减少和运动稳定性13%、22%和15个百分点，约1,800步收敛，端到端延迟7.4毫秒，在平地行走、楼梯、坡道和崎岖地形中均表现优异。

Conclusion: OLIVE证明了在线低秩增量学习可有效实现穿戴式外骨骼的实时个性化，在多种地形条件下实现鲁棒控制，为实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Wearable exoskeleton systems hold promise for restoring mobility in individuals with physical impairments, yet most existing controllers rely on static gait policies that lack the ability to adapt to dynamic real-world environments or individual user characteristics. We present \olive (\underline{O}nline \underline{L}ow-rank \underline{I}ncremental Learning for Efficient Adapti\underline{ve} Exoskeletons), a parameter-efficient online adaptation framework that continuously personalizes exoskeleton control during deployment. \olive decomposes the adaptive component of the control policy into a low-rank residual form~$\dW = \At\Bt^\top$ with rank~$r!\ll!\min(d,k)$, reducing online update cost from $\mathcal{O}(dk)$ to $\mathcal{O}(r(d{+}k))$ while preserving the stability of a pretrained base controller~$\Wz$. Parameters are updated via a reward-shaped policy gradient driven purely by on-body sensor feedback (EMG, IMU, vibration), eliminating dependence on offline reference trajectories. A gating mechanism modulates the strength of personalization based on contextual state, and a dynamic rank scheduler adapts the update dimensionality to terrain complexity -- allocating minimal capacity on simple flat terrain and expanding to higher-rank updates on demanding uneven surfaces -- enabling robust performance across diverse activities: flat walking, stair navigation, slopes, and uneven terrain. Experiments on the wearable platform demonstrate that \olive achieves +13, +22, and +15 percentage-point improvements in gait smoothness, effort reduction, and motion stability over the strongest baseline, converging within $\sim$1{,}800 walking steps at 7.4,ms end-to-end latency. Our code implementation is available at https://github.com/FastLM/OLIVE.

</details>


### [125] [Learning Contact Representation for Leg Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05501)
*Emre Girgin,Cagri Kilic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于关节编码器的自监督接触检测框架，用于腿部机器人的腿部阶段检测，无需力传感器即可实现高精度接触检测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿部机器人里程计依赖于足端相对于世界坐标系速度为零的假设，准确识别腿部阶段是关键。传统方法依赖足端安装的地反力传感器，但存在传感器不可用、对滑动等扰动不敏感等问题

Method: 提出自监督表示学习框架，仅使用关节编码器作为输入，利用学习到的表示对支撑相和摆动相进行概率建模，实现无传感器增强的接触检测

Result: 实验验证了所提自监督接触检测器的有效性，在性能上优于需要传感器增强和标注的监督方法以及基线概率方法

Conclusion: 框架表现出色，代码已开源，为腿部机器人的腿部阶段检测提供了无需力传感器的可靠解决方案

Abstract: The estimation of odometry in legged robots depends on the assumption that the velocity of the foot with respect to the world remains zero during the stance phase. Feedback for the main body velocity is derived from the kinematic serial chain of the feet making accurate leg phase detection is a critical subproblem. A considerable number of studies employ ground reaction force sensors mounted at the tip of the foot to classify, yet these sensors may not be universally available for all legged robots. Additionally, these sensors are often unresponsive to unaccounted disturbances, such as slippage, while the foot remains in contact with the ground. In this study, we propose a self-supervised representation learning framework for contact detection that utilizes the standard sensor set of joint encoders without reliance on force sensor augmentations. We employ learned representations to model the stance and swing phases probabilistically. The experimental results obtained confirm the efficacy of the proposed self-supervised contact detector. Our framework exhibited superior performance in comparison to supervised methods which necessitate sensor set augmentation and labeling, as well as baseline probabilistic approaches. Additionally, we make our code available to the public.

</details>


### [126] [Discrete-WAM: Unified Discrete Vision-Action Token Editing for World-Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05645)
*Ziyang Yao,Haochen Liu,Yuncheng Jiang,Zeyu Zhu,Zibin Guo,Jingru Wang,Tianle Liu,Jianwei Cui,Kuiyuan Yang,Hongwei Xie,Jingwei Zhao,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Discrete-WAM，一种统一潜在视觉-动作世界策略，通过离散token对齐未来视觉状态和自我动作，实现组合因果推理，支持世界建模、世界-动作策略和分层决策策略的联合建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需推理自我动作如何塑造周围世界的演化。现有端到端方法仅捕获相关性，未显式建模条件动力学；连续潜在世界模型缺乏组合结构，难以进行反事实因果推理。

Method: Discrete-WAM建立共享离散扩散框架，将未来视觉状态和自我动作表示为对齐的离散tokens，联合建模世界模型、世界-动作策略和分层决策策略，支持跨场景的组合泛化。

Result: 在大规模自动驾驶基准上取得竞争性能，支持可控生成和反事实推理，同时保持可控性。

Conclusion: 为自动驾驶更可靠的决策制定提供了原则性路径。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires reasoning about how ego actions shape the evolution of the surrounding world. However, most end-to-end methods rely on direct state-to-action mappings, capturing correlations without explicitly modeling action-conditioned dynamics. Conversely, continuous-latent world models often lack compositional structure for causal reasoning across counterfactual futures. We introduce Discrete-WAM, a unified latent vision-action world policy that represents future visual states and ego actions as aligned discrete tokens, enabling compositional causal reasoning across alternative futures. Built upon this unified discrete alignment, Discrete-WAM establishes a shared discrete diffusion framework with unified generative tasks, jointly formulating world modeling, world-action policy, and hierarchical decision-enabled policy, supporting compositional generalization across diverse driving scenarios. Experiments on large-scale autonomous-driving benchmarks show that Discrete-WAM achieves competitive performance while supporting controllable generation and counterfactual reasoning, offering a principled path toward more reliable decision-making.

</details>


### [127] [Visuotactile and Explicitly Force-Controlled Robotic Ultrasound for Abdominal Volumetric Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05848)
*Adrian Piedra,R Brooke Jeffrey,Oussama Khatib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种集成立体视觉、触觉反馈和专家策略的自主腹部超声扫描机器人系统，能够基于专家数据进行扫描路径规划，实现高质量三维体积成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决传统超声扫描依赖人工操作、难以实现标准化和自动化的问题，需要开发能够模仿专家扫描策略、自适应患者解剖结构的自主超声系统。

Method: 通过记录专家放射科医生的自由手部运动和力数据构建扫描框架；利用立体视觉生成患者腹部三维地形图，结合关键点刚度测量确定肋骨边界；规划两种扫描路径（肋骨下向上扫描和软组织垂直扫描）；采用7自由度柔性扭矩控制机械臂，通过闭环力控制保持探头接触。

Result: 实验表明系统实现了与专家相当的高质量成像，能够动态适应患者特定的解剖结构；通过三维体积采集能力超越了专家手工扫描的局限性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将专家知识融入自主机器人系统的可行性，展示了基于感知的自主性与物理推理相结合在提升诊断性能方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a robotic ultrasound acquisition system that integrates stereo vision, touch-based feedback, and expert-informed strategies to perform autonomous and adaptive abdominal scans. The system records freehand motion and force data from expert radiologists, creating a framework to capture transducer motion, applied forces, and anatomical scanning strategies. This expert data is replayed to replicate characteristic scans with the robot, forming a foundation for further autonomous capabilities. Using stereo vision, the system generates three-dimensional topography maps of the patient's abdomen, which are refined through stiffness measurements at key points to delineate the rib cage boundary. These combined techniques enable the robot to execute two distinct scanning paths: an upward-angled sweep beneath the rib cage to visualize structures near the upper abdomen and a perpendicular sweep across soft tissue regions. A compliant, torque-controlled seven degree-of-freedom robotic manipulator is controlled to maintain consistent probe contact through closed-loop force control over the varied anatomical surfaces. Physical experiments demonstrate that the system achieves high-quality imaging comparable to expert scans while dynamically adapting to patient-specific topographies. Furthermore, the robotic system surpasses expert capabilities by enabling three-dimensional volume acquisition, which enhances diagnostic potential and provides volumetric data for advanced analyses. This work highlights the integration of expert knowledge into autonomous robotic systems and underscores the potential of combining perception-based autonomy with physical reasoning for enhanced diagnostic performance.

</details>


### [128] [Towards a Data Flywheel for Embodied Intelligence in Logistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05960)
*Anlan Yu,Zaishu Chen,Zhiqing Hong,Daqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究构建物流行业的数据中心框架（物流数据飞轮），通过World Models合成分布外数据用于鲁棒的模仿学习，实现工业具身智能的规模化部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能从实验室走向工业部署，传统感知-规划-控制管道存在可扩展性瓶颈，物流行业是关键应用场景，需要解决具身数据的收集、组织和重用问题。

Method: 构建物流数据飞轮框架：1）将日常运营转化为可重用数据资产；2）使用World Models为长尾包裹操作生成可靠监督；3）将部署反馈用于策略改进；4）提出WM-DAgger框架合成分布外恢复数据用于鲁棒模仿学习；5）探索大规模野外多模态数据对齐。

Result: 提出WM-DAgger框架，通过世界模型合成分布外数据实现鲁棒模仿学习；正在探索大规模野外多模态数据（标注人类演示、未标注操作视频、系统级机器人日志）的对齐与转化。

Conclusion: 数据中心框架将日常运营数据转化为可重用资产，通过World Models和反馈循环实现持续系统改进，为工业具身智能的规模化部署提供数据基础。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence is moving from laboratory demonstrations toward industrial deployment, with the logistics industry serving as a key application scenario. Learning-based policies offer a promising path beyond traditional perception-planning-control pipelines, but their scalability depends on how embodied data can be collected, organized, and reused. This research studies a data-centric framework for industrial embodied intelligence by constructing a logistics data flywheel. Our framework converts daily operations into reusable data assets, uses World Models to generate reliable supervision for long-tail parcel manipulation, and feeds deployment feedback back into policy improvement. As an initial result, \textit{WM-DAgger} introduces a World-Model-based data aggregation framework that synthesizes out-of-distribution recovery data for robust imitation learning. Building on this result, ongoing work explores how large-scale in-the-wild multimodal data, including labeled human demonstrations, unlabeled operational videos, and system-level robot logs, can be aligned for policy learning and transformed into feedback for continual system improvement.

</details>


### [129] [A Conversational Framework for Human-Robot Collaborative Manipulation with Distributed Generative AI models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06061)
*Arash Ghasemzadeh Kakroudi,Roel Pieters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种分布式人机协作操作框架，将本地语言和视觉语言模型与ROS 2执行栈集成，支持灵活的分布式部署和响应式控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对人机协作操作中分布式硬件部署的灵活性需求和响应式控制循环的保持需求，研究如何构建能够处理自由形式用户指令并生成结构化动作请求（如抓取、放置、传递）的框架。

Method: 将语言理解、视觉定位、编排和运动执行作为独立的ROS 2节点运行；使用视觉语言模型（VLM）返回图像空间目标，通过深度信息和标定转换为机器人坐标系下的度量目标；通过Web仪表板展示中间意图和定位叠加层（像素、深度、机器人坐标系），并要求操作员在执行任何运动前明确确认；在Franka FR3平台上评估。

Result: 实验评估了端到端任务可靠性和延迟，在不同工作台场景模糊度增加条件下进行测试，并比较了相同管道中不同的LLM/VLM配置。

Conclusion: 框架实现了可靠的人机协作操作，代码已开源，支持跨分布式硬件的灵活部署和响应式控制。

Abstract: This paper presents a distributed conversational framework for human-robot collaborative manipulation that integrates local language and vision-language models (VLMs) with a Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2)-based execution stack. Language understanding, visual grounding, orchestration, and motion execution run as separate ROS 2 nodes, enabling flexible deployment across distributed hardware while maintaining a responsive control loop. From free-form user commands, the system generates structured action requests for pick, place, and handover. It uses a VLM to return image-space targets, which are converted into metric robot-frame goals using depth and calibration. A web dashboard exposes intermediate intent and grounding overlays (pixel, depth, and robot-frame) and requires explicit operator confirmation before any motion is executed. Experiments on a Franka FR3 platform evaluate end-to-end task reliability and latency under increasing working table scene ambiguity and compare alternative LLM/VLM configurations in the same pipeline. Code and full documentation are available at [github.com/cogrob-tuni/franka-llm](https://github.com/cogrob-tuni/franka-llm).

</details>


### [130] [CLEAR: Cognition and Latent Evaluation for Adaptive Routing in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06219)
*Yining Xing,Zehong Ke,Zhiyuan Liu,Yanbo Jiang,Wenhao Yu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLEAR 框架通过单步条件漂移和语义状态引导，在 NAVSIM v1 基准上实现 93.7 的 SOTA PDMS，解决了扩散模型在自动驾驶中的实时性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶模型难以平衡多模态规划与实时推理；扩散模型虽然能捕获多样化驾驶行为，但迭代去噪过程延迟过高，不适用于安全关键场景部署。

Method: 1) 采用 Drive-JEPA 视觉编码器；2) 用 VAE 潜空间单步条件漂移替代多步去噪；3) 引入条件系数 α 平衡多样性与专家精度；4) 微调 Qwen3.5-0.8B 提取场景感知隐藏状态；5) 自适应调度器选择 α 和 N；6) 跨注意力评分器选择最优轨迹。

Result: 在 NAVSIM v1 基准上达到 93.7 的 SOTA PDMS，验证了高保真多模态规划可在无密集几何标注和迭代采样下高效执行。

Conclusion: 单步生成与语义推理的结合能有效缓解实时性瓶颈，为安全关键自动驾驶部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models often struggle to balance multi-modal maneuver generation with real-time inference constraints. While diffusion models successfully capture diverse driving behaviors, their iterative denoising process incurs unacceptable latency for safety-critical deployment. To address this, we propose CLEAR (Cognition and Latent Evaluation for Adaptive Routing), a framework that combines ultra-fast generative planning with deep semantic reasoning. CLEAR employs Drive-JEPA as the visual encoder and replaces the multi-step denoising chain with a single-step conditional drift in a VAE latent space, introducing a conditioning coefficient to balance diversity and expert precision. Meanwhile, we fully fine-tune Qwen~3.5~0.8B on driving QA pairs to extract scene-aware hidden states. These states guide both an Adaptive Scheduler, which selects the conditioning coefficient $α$ and sample count $N$ from a discrete set of predefined schemes, and a cross-attention scorer that selects the optimal trajectory from candidates. On the NAVSIM v1 benchmark, CLEAR achieves a state-of-the-art PDMS of 93.7. Our results demonstrate that high-fidelity, multi-modal planning can be executed efficiently without dense geometric annotations or iterative sampling.

</details>


### [131] [MPCoT: Reward-Guided Multi-Path Latent Reasoning for Test-Time Scalable Vision-Language-Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06245)
*Boyang Zhang,Lianlei Shan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MPCoT是一种面向视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略的奖励引导多路径潜在推理框架，通过初始化M个假设并经K步权重关联细化后进行软聚合，在保持原8步动作接口且零推理token的前提下，提升长时域与高不确定性控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略在长时域和高不确定性控制中表现脆弱；单次动作解码推理深度有限，显式思维链引入token延迟并造成间接文本到动作接口；需要一种既能增强推理深度又不增加延迟与间接交互的推理机制。

Method: MPCoT初始化M个假设路径，经K步权重关联细化后进行软聚合；使用仅训练阶段的路径偏好目标，结合专家动作一致性、基于世界模型/VLM的进展指标与成功反馈，对潜在路径评分器进行对齐；保留原8步动作接口，不生成推理token，支持可配置的推理控制参数(K,M)。

Result: 在LIBERO与CALVIN的匹配协议下，MPCoT显著提升长时域性能；消融实验证实了深度-宽度效应、置信度加权聚合以及奖励引导路径监督的有效性。

Conclusion: MPCoT在保持原始动作接口与零推理token的前提下，引入可控的多路径潜在推理，增强了推理深度与执行质量，有效缓解长时域和高不确定性控制中的脆弱性问题。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies remain brittle in long-horizon and high-uncertainty control, where one-pass action decoding provides limited inference-time deliberation. Explicit chain-of-thought can increase reasoning depth, but introduces token latency and an indirect text-to-action interface. We propose MPCoT, a reward-guided multi-path latent reasoning framework that initializes $M$ hypotheses, refines them for K weight-tied steps, and softly aggregates them before action decoding. A training-only path-preference objective evaluates candidate action branches with expert-action consistency, world-model/VLM-based progress, and success feedback to align the latent path scorer with downstream execution quality. MPCoT preserves the original 8-step action interface, generates zero reasoning tokens, and exposes configurable inference controls (K,M). Under matched protocols on LIBERO and CALVIN, MPCoT improves long-horizon performance, with ablations confirming depth-width effects, confidence-weighted aggregation, and reward-guided path supervision.

</details>


### [132] [RadiusFPS: Efficient Farthest Point Sampling on CPUs and GPUs via Spherical Voxel Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06255)
*Ziyang Yu,Xiang Li,Qiong Chang,Jun Miyazaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Point clouds are a primary sensory representation for robotic perception, underpinning LiDAR-based autonomous driving, simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM), and navigation. Within these pipelines, Farthest Point Sampling (FPS) is the most well-known downsampling operator, as its uniform coverage preserves the geometric structure on which downstream perception relies. However, the large time complexity of classical FPS scales poorly with the million-point-per-second rates of modern 3D sensors, making it a dominant latency bottleneck that conflicts with the real-time and limited onboard compute budgets of robotic systems. Therefore, we propose RadiusFPS, an FPS acceleration framework based on spherical voxel pruning that preserves the standard FPS update rule under the same initialization and tie-breaking policy. By indexing the point cloud with spherical voxels, RadiusFPS derives a conservative geometric bound that prunes redundant distance computations in each iteration, complemented by a coordinate-wise point-skip test that removes residual updates. We further introduce RadiusFPS-G, a warp-level GPU implementation that fuses voxel selection, pruning, and distance update into memory-coalesced kernels, eliminating costly global-memory round-trips. On indoor (S3DIS, ScanNet) and outdoor LiDAR (SemanticKITTI) benchmarks, RadiusFPS-G attains up to 2.5x speedup over GPU-based FPS and matches or exceeds QuickFPS among the evaluated methods while using roughly half its GPU memory, with comparable segmentation accuracy. When coupled with the learning-based FastPoint sampler, the resulting pipeline achieves the fastest End-to-End inference among all evaluated configurations. These properties make high-quality FPS-style sampling practical for latency- and memory-constrained robotic vision.

</details>


### [133] [Meridian: Metric-Semantic Primitive Matching for Cross-View Geo-Localization Beyond Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06312)
*Mason Peterson,Qingyuan Li,Yixuan Jia,Fernando Cladera,Carlos Nieto-Granda,Camillo Jose Taylor,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Meridian是一种无需环境特定训练即可在GNSS受限环境下实现地面机器人全局定位的方法。该方法通过匹配航空图像与机器人RGB-D相机的度量语义图元，在多样化的自然环境（包括公园、校园、野外营地）中实现了平均2.4米的轨迹误差（19公里）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人自动化需要准确的全局定位来支持重复性任务、规划、目标指定和安全运行，但在GNSS受限环境中可靠定位仍是开放问题。现有方法主要针对结构化城市环境，在非结构化自然地形中很少得到验证，且难以处理重复几何结构和无特征景观。

Method: Meridian方法通过匹配航空图像与地面机器人RGB-D相机的度量语义图元实现全局定位。提出新颖的一致性指标来估计机器人子地图位姿的分布，并在鲁棒的位姿图优化步骤中剔除异常假设，从而实现准确的轨迹估计。该方法无需任何训练或算法微调即可跨多样化环境泛化。

Result: 该方法在自动驾驶数据集、公园校园区域和野外营地等多种环境中成功定位机器人，在19公里地面行程中平均优化轨迹误差为2.4米。实验证明算法能跨多种环境可靠运行。

Conclusion: Meridian方法能够在无需环境特定数据训练的情况下，在多样化的非结构化自然环境中实现准确的地面机器人全局定位，为GNSS受限环境下的机器人自主导航提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Successful robot automation requires accurate global localization to support repeatability, task planning, goal specification, and safe operation. However, reliable localization in GNSS-denied environments remains an open problem. Overhead aerial imagery offers a promising solution, but existing approaches primarily target structured urban environments and have been rarely demonstrated in unstructured natural terrain. Limitations of the state-of-the-art include a reliance on models trained for specific environments, as well as difficulty handling repetitive geometries and featureless landscapes commonly found in natural outdoor areas. To overcome these challenges, we present Meridian, a method for matching high-level metric-semantic primitives across aerial images and ground robot RGB-D camera data that achieves accurate global localization and generalizes well across diverse environments, all without any training or algorithmic fine-tuning on area-specific data. We formulate novel consistency metrics to estimate a distribution over robot submap poses and to reject outlier hypotheses in a robust pose graph optimization step for accurate robot trajectory estimation. We demonstrate that our algorithm can localize a ground robot across a wide variety of environments, including an autonomous driving dataset, a park and campus area, and a wilderness camp, with an average optimized trajectory error of 2.4 m over 19 km of ground traversal.

</details>


### [134] [Waypoints Matter: A Systematic Study for Sampling-Based Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06366)
*Josep M. Barbera,Antonio Artuñedo,Jorge Villagra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究系统比较了三种路点放置策略（均匀间隔、RDP*、曲率条件分配）在实时自主驾驶轨迹规划器中的性能，发现名义间隔距离是主要性能驱动因素，均匀采样在多数情况下表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时自主驾驶的采样式轨迹规划器性能高度依赖路点放置策略，但现有研究未充分探索路点放置对规划器存在性和质量的影响，需要系统评估不同放置策略的有效性。

Method: 保持轨迹原语和候选预算固定，在449种配置和5个CommonRoad地图上系统测试三种放置策略：均匀间隔、RDP*变体、曲率条件分配，评估规划器可靠性和性能差异。

Result: 名义间隔距离ds是主要性能驱动因素，均匀采样在适当调优间隔下匹配或超过其他策略；曲率条件分配在几何复杂道路上提供微小但一致的可靠性优势；RDP*从未优于均匀采样。

Conclusion: ds应作为主要调优参数，几何感知策略应保留在曲率丰富的走廊中作为可行性限制因素；对于大多数情况，简单的均匀间隔配合良好调优即可获得最佳性能。

Abstract: Real-time autonomous driving commonly relies on sampling-based trajectory planners that link candidate trajectories to target waypoints along the road centerline. The placement of these waypoints directly impacts both the existence and quality of feasible trajectories. Yet, its effect on planner performance remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we treat waypoint placement as a first-class design variable. We hold the trajectory primitive and candidate budget fixed, and systematically sweep three placement strategies (uniform spacing, an augmented Ramer-Douglas-Peucker variant (RDP*), and a novel curvature-conditioned allocation) across 449 configurations and five CommonRoad maps of increasing geometric complexity. Our results show that the nominal inter-waypoint spacing $d_s$ is the primary performance driver, with large differences in planner reliability attributed to placement alone. Uniform sampling at a well-tuned spacing matches or surpasses both RDP* and the centered curvature variant. The curvature variant offers a small but consistent advantage on geometrically complex roads under reliability-first and balanced weightings, while RDP* never outperforms uniform sampling. These findings suggest that $d_s$ should be treated as the dominant tuning parameter, with geometry-aware strategies reserved for curvature-rich corridors where feasibility is the limiting factor.

</details>


### [135] [RiskFlow: Fast and Faithful Safety-Critical Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06423)
*Qi Lan,Yining Tang,Yu Shen,Yi Zhou,Yuhao Wei,Jie Li,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RiskFlow是一种基于动作空间传输的闭环安全关键多智能体交通生成框架，通过学习平均速度场实现单次前向传递生成，显著提升生成真实性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的方法虽然闭环控制能力强，但迭代去噪过程计算昂贵且易在高时间步长中累积误差，导致生成场景出现抖动、异常加速度等不真实运动伪影。

Method: 1) 将未来轨迹生成 formulation 为动作空间中的传输；2) 学习有限区间内平均速度场将高斯动作序列转换为加速度和偏航率指令；3) 使用JVP目标进行高效稳定训练；4) 测试时应用输出空间引导使关键智能体朝向危险交互；5) 通过车辆动力学重建物理可行轨迹。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，RiskFlow实现了强对抗性与真实感的权衡，相比基线持续提升真实性同时保持安全关键生成能力，且大幅减少推理时间。

Conclusion: RiskFlow成功解决了扩散方法计算成本高和误差累积问题，在保持安全关键场景生成能力的同时，显著提升生成真实性和推理效率，为自动驾驶系统评估提供了更高效的工具。

Abstract: Safety-critical traffic scenario generation is essential for evaluating autonomous driving systems under rare but high-risk interactions. Existing diffusion-based methods offer strong controllability in closed-loop generation, but their iterative denoising process is computationally expensive and may accumulate sampling and guidance errors over long rollouts, causing unrealistic motion artifacts such as jitter, abnormal acceleration, and off-road behavior. To address these issues, we propose RiskFlow, a closed-loop safety-critical multi-agent traffic generation framework that formulates future trajectory generation as transport in the action space. Instead of relying on iterative denoising, RiskFlow learns an average velocity field over a finite interval to transform Gaussian action sequences into future acceleration and yaw-rate commands with a single forward pass, using a JVP-based objective for efficient and stable training. At test time, RiskFlow applies output-space guidance to the generated actions, steering selected critical agents toward risky interactions while regularizing off-road behavior, and reconstructs physically feasible trajectories through vehicle dynamics. Experiments on nuScenes with tbsim closed-loop evaluation show that RiskFlow achieves a strong adversariality-realism trade-off across multi-agent and long-horizon settings. Compared with representative baselines, RiskFlow consistently improves realism while maintaining competitive safety-critical generation capability, and substantially reduces inference time for evaluation.

</details>


### [136] [Ask-to-Clarify: Resolving Instruction Ambiguity through Multi-turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.15061)
*Xingyao Lin,Xinghao Zhu,Tianyi Lu,Sicheng Xie,Hui Zhang,Xipeng Qiu,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Ask-to-Clarify框架，通过多轮对话解决模糊指令并生成底层动作，结合VLM和扩散模型实现协作式具身智能体


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLA的具身智能体以单向模式运行，缺乏反馈机制，无法处理现实场景中模糊指令的问题，难以实现与人类协作的目标

Method: 框架包含VLM协作组件和扩散动作组件，通过连接模块将VLM输出转化为扩散模型的条件信号。采用两阶段知识隔离训练策略：先用模糊指令解决对话数据微调协作组件，再集成动作组件（冻结协作组件）。推理时使用信号检测器作为路由器在提问和行动间切换

Result: 在8个真实世界任务中评估，Ask-to-Clarify框架性能优于现有最先进的VLA方法

Conclusion: 提出的框架及训练策略为构建协作式具身智能体提供了可行路径

Abstract: The ultimate goal of embodied agents is to create collaborators that can interact with humans, not mere executors that passively follow instructions. This requires agents to communicate, coordinate, and adapt their actions based on human feedback. Recently, advances in VLAs have offered a path toward this goal. However, most current VLA-based embodied agents operate in a one-way mode: they receive an instruction and execute it without feedback. This approach fails in real-world scenarios where instructions are often ambiguous. In this paper, we address this problem with the Ask-to-Clarify framework. Our framework first resolves ambiguous instructions by asking questions in a multi-turn dialogue. Then it generates low-level actions end-to-end. Specifically, the Ask-to-Clarify framework consists of two components, one VLM for collaboration and one diffusion for action. We also introduce a connection module that generates conditions for the diffusion based on the output of the VLM. This module adjusts the observation by instructions to create reliable conditions. We train our framework with a two-stage knowledge-insulation strategy. First, we fine-tune the collaboration component using ambiguity-solving dialogue data to handle ambiguity. Then, we integrate the action component while freezing the collaboration one. This preserves the interaction abilities while fine-tuning the diffusion to generate actions. The training strategy guarantees our framework can first ask questions, then generate actions. During inference, a signal detector functions as a router that helps our framework switch between asking questions and taking actions. We evaluate the Ask-to-Clarify framework in 8 real-world tasks, where it outperforms existing state-of-the-art VLAs. The results suggest that our proposed framework, along with the training strategy, provides a path toward collaborative embodied agents.

</details>
